~~~python
    s_out = [1 if v[j] >= THETA else 0 for j in range(N_OUT)]
    v = [F(0) if s_out[j] else v[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    x = [st['x'][i] * DEC + s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)]
    y = [st['y'][j] * DEC + s_out[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    comp = {}
    for i in range(N_IN):
        for j in range(N_OUT):
            pos = A_PLUS * x[i] if (plastic and bap and s_out[j]) else F(0)
            neg = -A_MINUS * y[j] if (plastic and s_in[i] and bap) else F(0)
            rel = -relax * (w[i][j] - W0) if plastic else F(0)
            nw = min(W_MAX, max(F(0), w[i][j] + pos + neg + rel))
            comp[(i, j)] = (pos, neg, rel, nw - w[i][j])
            w[i][j] = nw
    ev = dict(inp=inp, teach=teach, s_in=s_in, s_out=tuple(s_out), comp=comp)
    return dict(w=w, v=v, x=x, y=y), ev

def run(hist, st=None, **kw):
    """hist：[(输入名, 教学电流)]。返回 (末态, 事件日志)。"""
    st = st or init()
    log = []
    for inp, teach in hist:
        st, ev = step(st, inp, teach, **kw)
        log.append(ev)
    return st, log

def log_hash(log):
    """冻结日志的指纹：事件按确定顺序序列化后取 SHA-256。"""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for ev in log:
        h.update(repr((ev['inp'], ev['teach'], ev['s_in'], ev['s_out'],
                       sorted((k, tuple(str(c) for c in v)) for k, v in ev['comp'].items()))).encode())
    return h.hexdigest()

def z(st):
    return [[st['w'][i][j] - W0 for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)]
~~~

A.4　gate\_class.py　与《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 附录 A.2 逐字相同：𝒦\_g 的定义 1—3 与 Gate.step

~~~python
"""事件门控类 𝒦_g 的形式定义（正文 3.1 定义 1—3）之独立实现，以及「冻结 SNN 是 𝒦_g 成员」的逐拍有限核验（正文命题 1）。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。运行：python3 gate_class.py
冻结 snn_core.py（附录 A.1）只作被核对象载入，载入前核 SHA-256。
K_g 成员 G 的参数（定义 1）：
  n_in, n_out          入口单元数、门控单元数；结构位 X = {(i, j)}（全连接）
  pat: Σ_in -> {0,1}^n_in  入口字母到入口脉冲向量（含静置 'none' -> 0 向量）
  lam[j], theta[j], vreset[j]   漏电、阈值、复位值
  dx[i], dy[j]         资格迹衰减
  Ap, Am, rho          增强幅度、抑制幅度、弛豫率
  b[i][j], lo[i][j], hi[i][j]   基线、合法区间
协议开关（申报项，不属状态）：plastic, bap
字母 σ = (入口名, I 向量)；I 为界外注入（教学电流）。
"""
import sys, hashlib, random, itertools, importlib.util
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
SNN_DIR = __import__('os').path.dirname(__import__('os').path.abspath(__file__))   # 冻结 snn_core.py 与本程序同目录（附录 A.1）
FROZEN_SHA = {'snn_core.py': '0c750fb5f57b52c40ff30feb7734c120096ffd7267e6ddc723d163cc0d1b4ceb'}

class Gate:
    """K_g 的一个成员（定义 1）。状态 o = (w, v, x, y)，w[i][j] 为结构位，v、x、y 为辅助位。"""
    def __init__(self, n_in, n_out, pat, lam, theta, vreset, dx, dy, Ap, Am, rho, b, lo, hi):
        self.n_in, self.n_out, self.pat = n_in, n_out, dict(pat)
        self.lam, self.theta, self.vreset = list(lam), list(theta), list(vreset)
        self.dx, self.dy = list(dx), list(dy)
        self.Ap, self.Am, self.rho = Ap, Am, rho
        self.b, self.lo, self.hi = b, lo, hi
        self.check_domain()
    def check_domain(self):
        """定义 1 的参数约束；不合即不是 K_g 成员。"""
        assert 'none' in self.pat and all(v == 0 for v in self.pat['none'])
        assert all(len(p) == self.n_in and all(c in (0, 1) for c in p) for p in self.pat.values())
        assert all(0 <= l <= 1 for l in self.lam) and all(0 <= d <= 1 for d in self.dx + self.dy)
        assert 0 <= self.rho <= 1 and self.Ap >= 0 and self.Am >= 0
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            for j in range(self.n_out):
                assert self.lo[i][j] <= self.b[i][j] <= self.hi[i][j]
        return True
    # ---------- 定义 2：按拍执行顺序（八阶段，读拍前状态） ----------
    def step(self, o, sigma, plastic=True, bap=True):
        """返回 (o', rec)。rec 列出门控实参 vt、g、各突触的 (P+, P-, Pr, 夹前和, 夹后值)。"""
        w, v, x, y = o
        name, I = sigma
        s = self.pat[name]                                                     # (1) 读入
        vt = [self.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(self.n_in)) + I[j]
              for j in range(self.n_out)]                                      # (2) 积分（拍前 w）
        g = [1 if vt[j] >= self.theta[j] else 0 for j in range(self.n_out)]    # (3) 门控，比较符 >=
        v2 = [self.vreset[j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(self.n_out)]    # (4) 复位
        x2 = [self.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(self.n_in)]              # (5) 资格迹
        y2 = [self.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(self.n_out)]
        w2 = [[None] * self.n_out for _ in range(self.n_in)]
        comp = {}
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            for j in range(self.n_out):
                pp = self.Ap * x2[i] if (plastic and bap and g[j]) else F(0)   # (6) 增强：仅 g_j = 1
                pm = -self.Am * y2[j] if (plastic and bap and s[i]) else F(0)  #     抑制：仅 s_i = 1
                pr = -self.rho * (w[i][j] - self.b[i][j]) if (plastic and self.rho != 0) else F(0)
                S = w[i][j] + pp + pm + pr
                nw = min(self.hi[i][j], max(self.lo[i][j], S))                 # (7) 夹限
                w2[i][j] = nw
                comp[(i, j)] = (pp, pm, pr, S, nw)
        rec = dict(s=s, I=tuple(I), vt=vt, g=tuple(g), comp=comp)              # (8) 读出 g
        return (w2, v2, x2, y2), rec
    def init(self, w=None):
        return ([[self.b[i][j] if w is None else w[i][j] for j in range(self.n_out)] for i in range(self.n_in)],
                [F(0)] * self.n_out, [F(0)] * self.n_in, [F(0)] * self.n_out)
    def run(self, hist, o=None, **kw):
        o = o or self.init()
        traj, recs = [o], []
        for sig in hist:
            o, r = self.step(o, sig, **kw)
            traj.append(o); recs.append(r)
        return traj, recs

def snn_params(S):
    """由 snn_core 模块当前的全局常数读出 K_g 参数（定义 1 与 snn_core 15—20 行的对应，正文命题 1）。"""
    n_in, n_out = S.N_IN, S.N_OUT
    full = lambda val: [[val] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
    return dict(n_in=n_in, n_out=n_out, pat=S.PAT, lam=[S.LEAK] * n_out, theta=[S.THETA] * n_out,
                vreset=[F(0)] * n_out, dx=[S.DEC] * n_in, dy=[S.DEC] * n_out, Ap=S.A_PLUS, Am=S.A_MINUS,
                b=full(S.W0), lo=full(F(0)), hi=full(S.W_MAX))

def load_snn_core():
    p = SNN_DIR + '/snn_core.py'
    h = hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()
    assert h == FROZEN_SHA['snn_core.py'], 'snn_core.py 不是冻结版本'
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('snn_core', p)
    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    return m, h

def snn_gate(S, rho=F(0)):
    return Gate(rho=rho, **snn_params(S))

def WIN0():
    return [('A', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('B', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 3

def compare_run(S, G, hist, w_init=None, o_init=None, plastic=True, bap=True, relax=F(0)):
    """同一史分别以 snn_core.step 与 G.step 运行，逐拍比较全状态与可塑性分项。
    返回 (拍数, 夹限事件数, 门控实参恰等于阈值的次数, 不一致列表)。"""
    if o_init is not None:
        st = dict(w=[r[:] for r in o_init[0]], v=list(o_init[1]), x=list(o_init[2]), y=list(o_init[3]))
        o = (o_init[0], o_init[1], o_init[2], o_init[3])
    else:
        st = S.init(w_init); o = G.init(w_init)
    bad, nclip, ntie = [], 0, 0
    for t, (inp, teach) in enumerate(hist):
        st, ev = S.step(st, inp, teach, plastic=plastic, bap=bap, relax=relax)
        o, r = G.step(o, (inp, teach), plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
        ntie += sum(1 for j in range(G.n_out) if r['vt'][j] == G.theta[j])
        if st['w'] != o[0] or st['v'] != o[1] or st['x'] != o[2] or st['y'] != o[3]:
            bad.append((t, 'state'))
        if tuple(ev['s_out']) != r['g'] or tuple(ev['s_in']) != tuple(r['s']):
            bad.append((t, 'spikes'))
        for k, (pp, pm, pr, Ssum, nw) in r['comp'].items():
            c = ev['comp'][k]
            w_before = Ssum - pp - pm - pr
            if (c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3]) != (pp, pm, pr, nw - w_before):
                bad.append((t, 'comp', k))
            if nw != Ssum:
                nclip += 1
    return len(hist), nclip, ntie, bad

def main():
    S, h = load_snn_core()
    print('一、冻结版本核验')
    print('  snn_core.py SHA-256 =', h, '（与冻结值相符）')
    base = {k: getattr(S, k) for k in ['LEAK', 'THETA', 'DEC', 'A_PLUS', 'A_MINUS', 'W0', 'W_MAX']}
    def setp(**kw):
        for k, val in base.items():
            setattr(S, k, kw.get(k, val))
    G = snn_gate(S)
    print('  定义 1 参数域：λ =', G.lam[0], '，d =', G.dx[0], '，ρ = 0（RELAX 缺省），A₊ =', G.Ap, '，A₋ =', G.Am,
          '，b =', G.b[0][0], '，[lo, hi] = [', G.lo[0][0], ',', G.hi[0][0], ']，θ =', G.theta[0], '，v_reset =',
          G.vreset[0], '；入口向量', sorted(G.pat.items()), '——约束全部满足：', G.check_domain())
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6
    CTRL = [('A', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6
    REST = [('none', (0, 0))] * 8
    WIN = WIN0()
    tot = dict(ticks=0, clip=0, tie=0, bad=[])
    def acc(res, label):
        n, c, ti, bad = res
        tot['ticks'] += n; tot['clip'] += c; tot['tie'] += ti; tot['bad'] += [(label,) + b for b in bad]
        return n, c, ti, len(bad)
    print('二、冻结申报史（snn_instance.py 所用）逐拍比较：K_g 定义 2 的 step 与 snn_core.step')
    rows = []
    rows.append(('FORM（形成期）', acc(compare_run(S, G, FORM), 'FORM')))
    rows.append(('CTRL（无教学对照）', acc(compare_run(S, G, CTRL), 'CTRL')))
    rows.append(('FORM+REST+WIN（有迹应）', acc(compare_run(S, G, FORM + REST + WIN), 'FRW')))
    st_r = S.run(REST, st=S.run(FORM)[0])[0]
    o_er = (S.init()['w'], st_r['v'], st_r['x'], st_r['y'])
    rows.append(('擦除侧 WIN（R1(a′)：权重复位，v、x、y 沿用静置末态，可塑性开）', acc(compare_run(S, G, WIN, o_init=o_er), 'ERASE')))
    o_r = (st_r['w'], st_r['v'], st_r['x'], st_r['y'])
    rows.append(('切断途径 WIN（bap 关）', acc(compare_run(S, G, WIN, o_init=o_r, bap=False), 'BAPOFF')))
    rows.append(('冻结可塑性 WIN（plastic 关）', acc(compare_run(S, G, WIN, o_init=o_r, plastic=False), 'FROZEN')))
    rows.append(('弛豫 RELAX = 1/10，FORM+REST+WIN', acc(compare_run(S, snn_gate(S, F(1, 10)), FORM + REST + WIN, relax=F(1, 10)), 'RELAX')))
    for lab, (n, c, ti, nb) in rows:
        print(f'  {lab}：{n} 拍，夹限 {c}，门控实参 = θ {ti} 次，不一致 {nb}')
    print('三、参数网格（snn_scan.py 的 108 组；按其方式改写 snn_core 全局常数，K_g 参数同步读出）')
    ng, tie_grid = 0, 0
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)],
                                              [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]):
        setp(W0=W0, A_PLUS=AP)
        Gp = snn_gate(S)
        form = ([('A', (teach, 0))] + [('none', (0, 0))] * 2) * n
        ctrl = ([('A', (0, 0))] + [('none', (0, 0))] * 2) * n
        tie_grid += acc(compare_run(S, Gp, form + REST + WIN0()), 'GRID')[2]
        tie_grid += acc(compare_run(S, Gp, ctrl), 'GRIDC')[2]
        ng += 1
    setp()
    print(f'  {ng} 组 × 2 条史完成；其中门控实参恰等于 θ 的拍 {tie_grid} 次（W0 = 1/2 时基线输入 1/2 + 1/2 = θ）')
    print('四、随机史（种子 20261101；300 条，长 60；字母含四种入口模式与两路教学电流 {0, 1/2, 1, 2}；')
    print('    初始权重随机取于 [0, 2] 的有理数；协议开关与 RELAX ∈ {0, 1/10, 1/2} 随机）')
    rng = random.Random(20261101)
    tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]
    for _ in range(300):
        rho = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 2)])
        Gr = snn_gate(S, rho)
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv))) for _ in range(60)]
        w0 = [[F(rng.randint(0, 16), 8) for _ in range(S.N_OUT)] for _ in range(S.N_IN)]
        acc(compare_run(S, Gr, hist, w_init=w0, plastic=rng.random() < 0.85, bap=rng.random() < 0.85, relax=rho), 'RAND')
    print('五、饱和史（检验夹限分支）：A_PLUS = 1/2、A_MINUS = 1/2，强教学配对与只前发放交替')
    setp(A_PLUS=F(1, 2), A_MINUS=F(1, 2))
    Gs = snn_gate(S)
    sat = ([('AB', (F(2), F(2)))] * 6 + [('A', (0, 0))] * 6 + [('B', (F(2), 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6) * 2
    n, c, ti, nb = acc(compare_run(S, Gs, sat), 'SAT')
    setp()
    print(f'  {n} 拍，夹限 {c}，不一致 {nb}')
    print('六、合计')
    print(f'  逐拍比较 {tot["ticks"]} 拍；夹限生效 {tot["clip"]} 处；门控实参恰等于阈值 {tot["tie"]} 次；不一致 {len(tot["bad"])} 处')
    print('  结论（有限核验）：在上列全部史上，K_g 定义 2 的按拍执行与 snn_core.step 逐拍逐分项精确相同，'
          '含夹限分支与等阈分支。' if not tot['bad'] else '  存在不一致：' + repr(tot['bad'][:10]))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.5　r\_layer\_witness.py　与《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 附录 A.3 逐字相同：R 层见证、槽位依赖图、引理 R1—R3

~~~python
"""𝒦_g 的《场具化数理式》R 层申报实例（正文 3.2 定义 4—7）：事件见证 Ev、槽位依赖图、引理 R1（K_t 闭合）、R2（读取集）、R3（不干涉）的有限核验，及由冻结 SNN 日志独立生成见证的逐拍核对。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。运行：python3 r_layer_witness.py
"""
import sys, random, io, contextlib
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
B, EXT = 'B', '界外'
HALF = F(1, 2)

def gate_reads(G, s, j):
    return frozenset([('v', j)] + [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) if s[i]])

def Ev(G, o, sigma, t, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """定义 5 的事件见证（a、κ、η 另行认证，见 gate_criteria.py）。只用拍前状态 o 与字母 σ。返回 {id: dict}。"""
    w, v, x, y = o
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    out = {}
    def add(kind, idx, car, tgt, c, reads):
        eid = (kind, idx, t)
        assert eid not in out
        out[eid] = dict(id=eid, t=t, car=car, tgt=tgt, c=c, reads=frozenset(reads))
    vt = [G.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
    g = [1 if vt[j] >= G.theta[j] else 0 for j in range(G.n_out)]
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        gr = gate_reads(G, s, j)
        add('LEAK', (j,), B, ('v', j), (G.lam[j] - 1) * v[j], {('v', j)})
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            if s[i]:
                add('SYN', (i, j), B, ('v', j), w[i][j], {('w', i, j)})
        if I[j] != 0:
            add('INJ', (j,), EXT, ('v', j), F(I[j]), set())
        if g[j]:
            add('RESET', (j,), B, ('v', j), G.vreset[j] - vt[j], gr)
            add('YIN', (j,), B, ('y', j), F(1), gr)
        add('YDEC', (j,), B, ('y', j), (G.dy[j] - 1) * y[j], {('y', j)})
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        add('XDEC', (i,), B, ('x', i), (G.dx[i] - 1) * x[i], {('x', i)})
        if s[i]:
            add('XIN', (i,), B, ('x', i), F(1), set())
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            gr = gate_reads(G, s, j)
            S = w[i][j]
            if plastic and bap and g[j]:
                c = G.Ap * (G.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i]); S += c
                add('PLUS', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), c, gr | {('x', i)})
            if plastic and bap and s[i]:
                c = -G.Am * (G.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j]); S += c
                add('MINUS', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), c, gr | {('y', j)})
            if plastic and G.rho != 0:
                c = -G.rho * (w[i][j] - G.b[i][j]); S += c
                add('RELAX', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), c, {('w', i, j)})
            cl = min(G.hi[i][j], max(G.lo[i][j], S))
            if cl != S:
                add('CLIP', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), cl - S, gr | {('w', i, j), ('x', i), ('y', j)})
    return out

def slot_deps(G, sigma, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """定义 7：槽位依赖。对给定字母（不依状态），返回 {后态结点 n: 前态结点集}，即 n(t+1) 所依的一切拍前结点：
    指向 n 的一切事件槽位（发生与否由状态定）的读取集之并，结构位另加自身（持存边）。"""
    s = G.pat[sigma[0]]
    dep = {}
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        gr = set(gate_reads(G, s, j))
        dep[('v', j)] = set(gr)                 # LEAK 读 v；SYN 读活跃 w；RESET 槽读 gr
        dep[('y', j)] = {('y', j)} | gr         # YDEC 读 y；YIN 槽读 gr
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        dep[('x', i)] = {('x', i)}
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            d = {('w', i, j)}                   # 持存
            if plastic and bap:
                d |= set(gate_reads(G, s, j)) | {('x', i)}   # PLUS 槽
                if s[i]:
                    d |= {('y', j)}                          # MINUS 槽
            d |= {('x', i), ('y', j)} if plastic and bap else set()   # CLIP 槽（并集上界）
            dep[('w', i, j)] = d
    return dep

def slot_reads(G, e):
    """事件 e 的槽位读取集（发生与分项所依的拍前结点；与 Ev 给出的 reads 相同，单列以示其只依字母）。"""
    return e['reads']

def node_val(o, k):
    w, v, x, y = o
    return w[k[1]][k[2]] if k[0] == 'w' else {'v': v, 'x': x, 'y': y}[k[0]][k[1]]

def nodes(G):
    return [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)] + [('v', j) for j in range(G.n_out)] + \
           [('x', i) for i in range(G.n_in)] + [('y', j) for j in range(G.n_out)]

def set_nodes(o, assign):
    """把 assign = {结点: 值} 写入状态（返回新状态）。"""
    w = [r[:] for r in o[0]]; v, x, y = list(o[1]), list(o[2]), list(o[3])
    for k, val in assign.items():
        if k[0] == 'w': w[k[1]][k[2]] = val
        elif k[0] == 'v': v[k[1]] = val
        elif k[0] == 'x': x[k[1]] = val
        else: y[k[1]] = val
    return (w, v, x, y)

def apply_events(G, o, evs):
    """K_t 的申报形式：后态 = 前态 + 同一结点上全部事件分项之和（引理 R1）。"""
    w, v, x, y = o
    w2 = [r[:] for r in w]; v2, x2, y2 = list(v), list(x), list(y)
    for e in evs.values():
        k = e['tgt']
        if k[0] == 'w': w2[k[1]][k[2]] += e['c']
        elif k[0] == 'v': v2[k[1]] += e['c']
        elif k[0] == 'x': x2[k[1]] += e['c']
        else: y2[k[1]] += e['c']
    return (w2, v2, x2, y2)

def ev_from_log(G, o, ev, t, plastic, bap, rho):
    """由 snn_core 日志（s_in, s_out, comp, teach）与拍前状态独立生成见证：发生与否取日志的发放与协议开关，
    可塑性分项取日志 comp，夹限事件取 comp 净值与三分项和之差。"""
    w, v, x, y = o
    s, g, I = ev['s_in'], ev['s_out'], ev['teach']
    out = {}
    def add(kind, idx, car, tgt, c):
        out[(kind, idx, t)] = dict(id=(kind, idx, t), t=t, car=car, tgt=tgt, c=c)
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        vt = G.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j]
        add('LEAK', (j,), B, ('v', j), (G.lam[j] - 1) * v[j])
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            if s[i]: add('SYN', (i, j), B, ('v', j), w[i][j])
        if I[j] != 0: add('INJ', (j,), EXT, ('v', j), F(I[j]))
        if g[j]:
            add('RESET', (j,), B, ('v', j), G.vreset[j] - vt); add('YIN', (j,), B, ('y', j), F(1))
        add('YDEC', (j,), B, ('y', j), (G.dy[j] - 1) * y[j])
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        add('XDEC', (i,), B, ('x', i), (G.dx[i] - 1) * x[i])
        if s[i]: add('XIN', (i,), B, ('x', i), F(1))
    for (i, j), (pp, pm, pr, net) in ev['comp'].items():
        if plastic and bap and g[j]: add('PLUS', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), pp)
        if plastic and bap and s[i]: add('MINUS', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), pm)
        if plastic and rho != 0: add('RELAX', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), pr)
        if net != pp + pm + pr: add('CLIP', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), net - (pp + pm + pr))
    return out

def d13_edges(G, evs, t):
    """D13：本拍实际证据路径图的边（读边 (n, t) → e、写边 e → (tgt, t+1)、结构位持存边 (w, t) → (w, t+1)），带时刻。"""
    E = []
    for e in evs.values():
        for k in e['reads']:
            E.append(((k, F(t)), (e['id'], F(t) + HALF)))
        E.append(((e['id'], F(t) + HALF), (e['tgt'], F(t + 1))))
    for k in nodes(G):
        if k[0] == 'w':
            E.append(((k, F(t)), (k, F(t + 1))))
    return E

def check_history(S, Gx, hist, o0=None, plastic=True, bap=True, rho=F(0), T=None):
    st = S.init() if o0 is None else dict(w=[r[:] for r in o0[0]], v=list(o0[1]), x=list(o0[2]), y=list(o0[3]))
    o = (st['w'], st['v'], st['x'], st['y'])
    stats = dict(ticks=0, events=0, clip=0, closure_bad=0, gen_bad=0, d4_bad=0, d13_bad=0, edges=0, d7_bad=0)
    all_ids = set()
    steps = []
    N = set(nodes(Gx))
    for t, (inp, teach) in enumerate(hist):
        evs = Ev(Gx, o, (inp, teach), t, plastic, bap)
        st, ev = S.step(st, inp, teach, plastic=plastic, bap=bap, relax=rho)
        o_next = (st['w'], st['v'], st['x'], st['y'])
        evl = ev_from_log(Gx, o, ev, t, plastic, bap, rho)
        if set(evs) != set(evl) or any(evs[k]['c'] != evl[k]['c'] or evs[k]['car'] != evl[k]['car'] or
                                       evs[k]['tgt'] != evl[k]['tgt'] for k in evs):
            stats['gen_bad'] += 1
        if apply_events(Gx, o, evs) != o_next:
            stats['closure_bad'] += 1
        for e in evs.values():   # D4：标识互异、载体值域、单一作用对象属结点集、时刻
            if e['id'] in all_ids or e['car'] not in (B, EXT) or e['tgt'] not in N or e['t'] != t:
                stats['d4_bad'] += 1
            all_ids.add(e['id'])
        for (u, tu), (vv, tv) in d13_edges(Gx, evs, t):   # D13：边按时间前向，端点类型合法
            stats['edges'] += 1
            okU = (u in N) or (u in evs)
            okV = (vv in N) or (vv in evs)
            if not (tu < tv) or not okU or not okV:
                stats['d13_bad'] += 1
        Omega = [dict(id=e['id'], t=t, car=e['car'], tgt=e['tgt'], c=e['c'], a=None, kappa=None, eta=None) for e in evs.values()]
        step = dict(t=t, o_before=o, o_after=o_next, Omega=Omega, Pi=[])   # D7：Π_t 为空（无作用于结构位的脉冲）
        if any(len(om) != 8 for om in Omega) or step['Pi']:
            stats['d7_bad'] += 1
        steps.append(step)
        stats['events'] += len(evs); stats['clip'] += sum(1 for e in evs.values() if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP')
        stats['ticks'] += 1
        o = o_next
    rpath = dict(X=[k for k in nodes(Gx) if k[0] == 'w'], Trace=None, Ident=None, steps=steps, T=T)
    return stats, rpath

def readset_check(G_list, n=1500, seed=7):
    """引理 R2 的有限核验：扰动读取集之外的全部结点为随机合法值，事件的发生与分项不变（夹限事件一并检查）。"""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]
    trials, viol, clip_trials = 0, 0, 0
    for _ in range(n):
        rnd = lambda hi_: F(rng.randint(0, 32), 16) * hi_ / 2
        o = ([[rnd(2) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)], [rnd(3) for _ in range(2)], [rnd(2) for _ in range(4)], [rnd(2) for _ in range(2)])
        sig = (rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv)))
        Gx = rng.choice(G_list)
        evs = Ev(Gx, o, sig, 0)
        for eid, e in evs.items():
            keep = e['reads']
            o2 = set_nodes(o, {k: (rnd(3) if k[0] == 'v' else rnd(2)) for k in nodes(Gx) if k not in keep})
            evs2 = Ev(Gx, o2, sig, 0)
            trials += 1
            clip_trials += eid[0] == 'CLIP'
            if eid not in evs2 or evs2[eid]['c'] != e['c']:
                viol += 1
    return trials, clip_trials, viol

def reach_forward(G, hist, sources, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """定义 7 的前向可达：sources = {(结点, 拍)}；返回 R[t] = 拍 t 拍前的可达结点集（含该拍的源）。"""
    R = {}
    cur = set()
    for t in range(len(hist) + 1):
        cur = set(cur) | {k for (k, u) in sources if u == t}
        R[t] = frozenset(cur)
        if t == len(hist):
            break
        dep = slot_deps(G, hist[t], plastic, bap)
        cur = {n for n, d in dep.items() if d & R[t]}
    return R

def noninterference_check(G_list, n=400, seed=11):
    """引理 R3 的有限核验：任取源结点集 S（任意结点、任意拍），在 S 上施加任意赋值；
    前向可达集之外的结点值与读取集在可达集之外的事件（发生与分项）都不变。"""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]
    stats = dict(runs=0, node_checks=0, ev_checks=0, viol=0)
    for _ in range(n):
        Gx = rng.choice(G_list)
        L = rng.randint(4, 16)
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv))) for _ in range(L)]
        o0 = ([[F(rng.randint(0, 16), 8) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)], [F(0)] * 2, [F(0)] * 4, [F(0)] * 2)
        srcs = {(rng.choice(nodes(Gx)), rng.randint(0, L - 1)) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 4))}
        vals = {sk: (F(rng.randint(0, 24), 8)) for sk in srcs}
        R = reach_forward(Gx, hist, srcs)
        o, oc = o0, o0
        for t in range(L):
            oc = set_nodes(oc, {k: vals[(k, u)] for (k, u) in srcs if u == t})
            for k in nodes(Gx):
                if k not in R[t]:
                    stats['node_checks'] += 1
                    if node_val(o, k) != node_val(oc, k):
                        stats['viol'] += 1
            ea, ec = Ev(Gx, o, hist[t], t), Ev(Gx, oc, hist[t], t)
            for eid in set(ea) | set(ec):
                rd = (ea.get(eid) or ec.get(eid))['reads']
                if not (rd & R[t]):
                    stats['ev_checks'] += 1
                    if (eid in ea) != (eid in ec) or (eid in ea and ea[eid]['c'] != ec[eid]['c']):
                        stats['viol'] += 1
            o, _ = Gx.step(o, hist[t]); oc, _ = Gx.step(oc, hist[t])
        stats['runs'] += 1
    return stats

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    EPS = F(1, 100)
    T = [('w', 0, 0), ('w', 1, 0)]
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6
    REST = [('none', (0, 0))] * 8
    WIN = GC.WIN0()
    HIST = FORM + REST + WIN
    t0 = len(FORM) + len(REST)
    print('一、申报（正文定义 4—7）')
    print('  O_t = (w, v, x, y)；结构位 X = 8 个突触；T = {w00, w10}（入口 0、1 → 输出 0）；ε =', EPS)
    print('  事件种类：LEAK_j、SYN_ij、INJ_j（界外）、RESET_j、YIN_j、YDEC_j、XDEC_i、XIN_i、PLUS_ij、MINUS_ij、RELAX_ij、CLIP_ij')
    print('  申报史：FORM(18) + REST(8) + WIN(18)，W = 拍', t0, '至', t0 + len(WIN) - 1, '（0 起计）；Π_t = ∅（无界外脉冲作用于结构位）')
    print('二、由 snn_core 运行日志生成 R 层见证并逐拍核对（引理 R1 的 K_t 一致、D4、D7、D13）')
    tot = []
    def show(label, stt):
        print(f'  {label}：{stt["ticks"]} 拍，事件见证 {stt["events"]}（夹限 {stt["clip"]}），D13 边 {stt["edges"]}；'
              f'两路生成不一致 {stt["gen_bad"]}；K_t 不一致 {stt["closure_bad"]}；D4 违例 {stt["d4_bad"]}；'
              f'D7 违例 {stt["d7_bad"]}；D13 违例 {stt["d13_bad"]}')
        tot.append(stt)
    stt, rpath = check_history(S, G, HIST, T=T); show('s1 冻结申报史', stt)
    st_r = S.run(REST, st=S.run(FORM)[0])[0]
    o_er = (S.init()['w'], st_r['v'], st_r['x'], st_r['y'])
    show('擦除侧 WIN（R1(a′)）', check_history(S, G, WIN, o0=o_er)[0])
    o_r = (st_r['w'], st_r['v'], st_r['x'], st_r['y'])
    show('bap 关', check_history(S, G, WIN, o0=o_r, bap=False)[0])
    show('plastic 关', check_history(S, G, WIN, o0=o_r, plastic=False)[0])
    G10 = GC.snn_gate(S, F(1, 10))
    show('RELAX = 1/10', check_history(S, G10, HIST, rho=F(1, 10))[0])
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 2), F(1, 2)
    Gs = GC.snn_gate(S)
    sat = ([('AB', (F(2), F(2)))] * 6 + [('A', (0, 0))] * 6 + [('B', (F(2), 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6) * 2
    show('饱和史（A_PLUS = A_MINUS = 1/2）', check_history(S, Gs, sat)[0])
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 8), F(1, 16)
    rng = random.Random(1101)
    tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]
    agg = dict(ticks=0, events=0, clip=0, closure_bad=0, gen_bad=0, d4_bad=0, d13_bad=0, edges=0, d7_bad=0)
    for _ in range(100):
        rho = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10)])
        Gr = GC.snn_gate(S, rho)
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv))) for _ in range(50)]
        w0 = [[F(rng.randint(0, 16), 8) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)]
        o0 = (w0, [F(0)] * 2, [F(0)] * 4, [F(0)] * 2)
        stt_r, _ = check_history(S, Gr, hist, o0=o0, plastic=rng.random() < .9, bap=rng.random() < .9, rho=rho)
        for k in agg: agg[k] += stt_r[k]
    show('随机史 100 条（种子 1101）', agg)
    print(f'  RPath（s1）：X = 8 位，T = {T}，见证步 {len(rpath["steps"])} 个；Trace、Ident 为证据值，由 gate_criteria.py 求出')
    print('三、读取集充分性（引理 R2；扰动读取集之外的全部结点为随机值；种子 7；含夹限事件）')
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 2), F(1, 2)
    Gsat = GC.snn_gate(S)
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 8), F(1, 16)
    trials, ctr, viol = readset_check([G, G10, Gsat])
    print(f'  {trials} 次事件—扰动对（其中夹限事件 {ctr} 次），违例 {viol}')
    print('四、不干涉引理（引理 R3）：槽位依赖图前向可达集之外，任意赋值干预不改变结点值与事件（种子 11）')
    ni = noninterference_check([G, G10, Gsat])
    print(f'  {ni["runs"]} 次随机史与随机源集；结点比较 {ni["node_checks"]}，事件比较 {ni["ev_checks"]}，违例 {ni["viol"]}')
    bad = sum(s_['closure_bad'] + s_['gen_bad'] + s_['d4_bad'] + s_['d13_bad'] + s_['d7_bad'] for s_ in tot)
    print('五、结论（有限核验）：', '全部史上 K_t 逐拍一致、两路见证相同、D4、D7、D13 无违例；读取集充分性与不干涉均无违例。'
          if bad == 0 and viol == 0 and ni['viol'] == 0 else f'存在违例 {bad} / {viol} / {ni["viol"]}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.6　gate\_criteria.py　与《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 附录 A.4 逐字相同：申报实例、合法设定运行与判词路径的求值库

~~~python
"""门控类的基本求值库：申报实例 Instance（形成记录、可用位）、合法设定运行、实际事件图与槽位可达、第三篇判词路径与 F 编码读数的求值器（正文 3.6、7.5 的对照用）。
库模块，不单独运行。只用标准库，精确有理运算。
"""
import sys
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
EPS = F(1, 100)
TPOS = [('w', 0, 0), ('w', 1, 0)]
NONE = ('none', (0, 0))
SAT, COV, PROF, UNDEF, RDEF = '⊥_Sat', '⊥_Cov', '⊥_Prof', '⊥_Undef', '⊥_Rdef'

def fr(q):
    return f'{q}' if q.denominator < 10 ** 6 else f'{float(q):.6f}'

def k_and(*vals):
    if 0 in vals: return 0
    if any(isinstance(v, str) for v in vals): return '⊥'
    return 1

def k_or(vals):
    vals = list(vals)
    if 1 in vals: return 1
    if any(isinstance(v, str) for v in vals): return '⊥'
    return 0

# ---------------- 运行与干预 ----------------
def run_with(G, o0, hist, t_start=0, interv=None, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """自拍 t_start 起按 hist 运行；interv(t, o) 在每拍执行前返回（可能被设定的）拍前状态。"""
    pre, evs, gates = {}, {}, {}
    o = o0
    for n, sig in enumerate(hist):
        t = t_start + n
        if interv is not None:
            o = interv(t, o)
        e = RW.Ev(G, o, sig, t, plastic, bap)
        o2, rec = G.step(o, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
        pre[t], evs[t], gates[t] = o, e, rec['g']
        o = o2
    pre[t_start + len(hist)] = o
    return pre, evs, gates

def setw(o, assign):
    w = [r[:] for r in o[0]]
    for (_, i, j), val in assign.items():
        w[i][j] = val
    return (w, o[1], o[2], o[3])

def zk(G, o, k):
    return o[0][k[1]][k[2]] - G.b[k[1]][k[2]]

class Instance:
    """申报实例：G、史、窗 W = [t0, len(hist)−1]、T、协议开关。入口拍 t0 − 1 在史内。"""
    def __init__(self, name, G, hist, t0, T=TPOS, plastic=True, bap=True, o0=None):
        self.name, self.G, self.hist, self.t0, self.T = name, G, hist, t0, T
        self.t1 = len(hist) - 1
        self.kw = dict(plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
        self.o0 = o0 or G.init()
        self.pre, self.evs, self.gates = run_with(G, self.o0, hist, **self.kw)
        self.form = {k: [] for k in T}     # D16（逐分项越阈）；R 层沿用
        for t in range(len(hist)):
            for e in self.evs[t].values():
                k = e['tgt']
                if k in T and abs(e['c']) > EPS and zk(G, self.pre[t], k) <= EPS < zk(G, self.pre[t], k) + e['c']:
                    self.form[k].append(t)
        self.avail = {k: [t for t in range(len(hist) + 1)
                          if zk(G, self.pre[t], k) > EPS and any(u < t for u in self.form[k])] for k in T}
        self._cf = None
        self._R = None
    def is_avail(self, k, t):
        return t in self.avail[k]
    def Wevents(self):
        return [e for t in range(self.t0, self.t1 + 1) for e in self.evs[t].values()]
    # ---- 合法设定运行（正判候选）与包络钉位（负判） ----
    def cf(self):
        if self._cf is not None:
            return self._cf
        G, T = self.G, self.T
        runs = []
        av_any = [t for k in T for t in self.avail[k] if t <= self.t1]
        if not av_any:
            self._cf = (runs, {}, None)
            return self._cf
        first = min(av_any)
        for u in range(first, self.t1 + 1):
            av = [k for k in T if self.is_avail(k, u)]
            if not av:
                continue
            for ks in [[k] for k in av] + ([av] if len(av) > 1 else []):
                for lab in ('b', 'hi'):
                    assign = {k: {'b': G.b, 'hi': G.hi}[lab][k[1]][k[2]] for k in ks}
                    if all(assign[k] == self.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]] for k in ks):
                        continue
                    pre, evs, gates = run_with(G, setw(self.pre[u], assign), self.hist[u:], t_start=u, **self.kw)
                    runs.append(dict(u=u, ks=ks, lab=lab, evs=evs, gates=gates))
        pins = {}
        for lab in ('hi', 'lo'):
            def interv(t, o, lab=lab):
                a = {}
                for k in T:
                    if self.is_avail(k, t):
                        if lab == 'hi':
                            a[k] = G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]
                        elif o[0][k[1]][k[2]] > G.b[k[1]][k[2]]:
                            a[k] = G.b[k[1]][k[2]]
                return setw(o, a) if a else o
            pre, evs, gates = run_with(G, self.pre[first], self.hist[first:], t_start=first, interv=interv, **self.kw)
            flip = [t for t in gates if gates[t] != self.gates[t]]
            pins[lab] = dict(evs=evs, gates=gates, first_flip=min(flip) if flip else None)
            runs.append(dict(u=first, ks=list(T), lab='钉位' + lab, evs=evs, gates=gates))
        self._cf = (runs, pins, first)
        return self._cf
    def reach(self):
        if self._R is None:
            srcs = {(k, u) for k in self.T for u in self.avail[k] if u <= self.t1}
            self._R = RW.reach_forward(self.G, self.hist, srcs, **self.kw)
        return self._R

def same_event(evsB, e):
    t, eid = e['t'], e['id']
    if t not in evsB:
        return True
    if eid not in evsB[t]:
        return False
    return evsB[t][eid]['c'] == e['c']

def covered(I, e, first=None):
    """引理 C 覆盖的事件种类：其发生与分项只依门控序列、入口字母、x、y 与 T 外结构位。
    引理 C′（扩展）：门控序列不变时，v_j(t) 只经「上次复位之后、t 之前」各拍对 T 中指向 j 之位的读取而依 T；
    该区间（并截于首个可设拍之后）内无此种读取时，LEAK_j@t 覆盖；RESET_j@t 另要求拍 t 本身也无此种读取。"""
    kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
    Tset = set(I.T)
    tj = {k[2] for k in I.T}
    if kind in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'YIN', 'YDEC', 'XDEC', 'XIN', 'INJ'):
        return True
    if kind in ('SYN', 'RELAX', 'CLIP'):
        return ('w', idx[0], idx[1]) not in Tset
    if kind in ('LEAK', 'RESET'):
        j = idx[0]
        if j not in tj:
            return True
        if first is None or not hasattr(I, 'gates'):
            return False
        t = e['t']
        last = max([u for u in range(t) if I.gates[u][j]], default=-1)
        lo_t = max(last, first - 1)
        hi_t = t if kind == 'RESET' else t - 1
        for u in range(lo_t + 1, hi_t + 1):
            s = I.G.pat[I.hist[u][0]]
            if any(s[k[1]] for k in I.T if k[2] == j):
                return False
        return True
    return False

def dep(I, e):
    runs, pins, first = I.cf()
    t = e['t']
    for r in sorted(runs, key=lambda r: -r['u']):
        if r['u'] <= t and not same_event(r['evs'], e):
            return 1, f"LOC 拍{r['u']} {','.join(f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}' for k in r['ks'])}←{r['lab']}"
    if not pins:
        return 0, '无可用位'
    if covered(I, e, first) and all(pins[l]['first_flip'] is None or pins[l]['first_flip'] > t for l in ('hi', 'lo')):
        return 0, '引理 C（两钉位至该拍无门控翻转）'
    return COV, '钉位翻转或种类不在引理 C 覆盖内，所试设定未改变该事件'

def path(I, e):
    R = I.reach()
    return 1 if (e['reads'] & R[e['t']]) else 0

def eta(I, e):
    """η（或 κ）的判值与依据。返回 (值, 依据, Dep 值, Path 值, 超定标记)。"""
    p = path(I, e)
    if p == 0:
        d = (0, '无途径')
        return 0, '无途径（引理 R3）', 0, 0, False
    dv, why = dep(I, e)
    if dv == 1:
        return 1, why, 1, 1, False
    if dv == 0:
        return 0, '超定：有途径而无依赖（' + why + '）', 0, 1, True
    return COV, why, dv, 1, False

def ko_act(G, o, evs, eid, eps=EPS):
    """引理 A：KO 认证的 a。返回 (a, KO 差)。"""
    e = evs[eid]; k = e['tgt']
    if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP':
        return None, None
    if k[0] != 'w':
        d = e['c']
        return (1 if abs(d) > eps else 0), d
    i, j = k[1], k[2]
    terms = [x['c'] for x in evs.values() if x['tgt'] == k and x['id'][0] != 'CLIP']
    S = o[0][i][j] + sum(terms)
    S_ko = S - e['c']
    clip = lambda z: min(G.hi[i][j], max(G.lo[i][j], z))
    inside = lambda z: G.lo[i][j] <= z <= G.hi[i][j]
    d = clip(S) - clip(S_ko)
    if inside(S) and inside(S_ko):
        return (1 if abs(d) > eps else 0), d
    return (1 if abs(d) > eps else SAT), d

def evaluate(I, part_all=False):
    """逐项求值本篇判据。part_all=True 时 Part 逐一求出全部 B 内事件的 κ（用于统计），否则遇见证即止。"""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    rows, hd, hd_cons = [], [], []
    n_over = 0
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if e['tgt'] not in T:
            continue
        a, d = ko_act(G, I.pre[e['t']], I.evs[e['t']], e['id'])
        if a is None:
            a = 1 if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0   # 夹限事件不作敲除对象；其作用由分项判（分项判据，一般式 105 行）
        ev, why, dv, pv, over = eta(I, e)
        n_over += over and a == 1
        rows.append(dict(e=e, a=a, d=d, eta=ev, why=why, dep=dv, path=pv, over=over))
        hd.append(k_and(a, ev))
        hd_cons.append(k_and(a, '⊥_Over' if over else ev))
    part, part_cons, wit, nk = [], [], None, 0
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if e['car'] != RW.B:
            continue
        kv, why, dv, pv, over = eta(I, e)
        nk += 1
        part.append(kv); part_cons.append('⊥_Over' if over else kv)
        if kv == 1 and wit is None:
            wit = (e['id'], why)
            if not part_all:
                break
    end = I.pre[I.t1 + 1]
    q = {k: (int(zk(G, end, k) > EPS), int(bool(I.form[k])), zk(G, end, k)) for k in T}
    qv = k_and(*[k_and(r, f) for (r, f, _) in q.values()])
    trace = k_and(RDEF, qv, 1, 1)      # 本求值器不含 r 的 R 层规则（r 由附录 A.14 求）；Ident（POS）、HistEv（REC）由模型结构为 1
    out = dict(rows=rows, HDyn=k_or(hd), HDyn_cons=k_or(hd_cons), Part=k_or(part), Part_cons=k_or(part_cons),
               Part_wit=wit, n_over=n_over, q=q, qv=qv, Trace=trace)
    out['Q'] = k_and(trace, out['Part'], out['HDyn'])
    runs, pins, first = I.cf()
    out['pins'] = {l: pins[l]['first_flip'] for l in pins} if pins else {}
    out['nruns'] = len(runs)
    out['Wmax_z'] = {k: max(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2)) for k in T}
    out['Wmin_z'] = {k: min(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2)) for k in T}
    return out

# ---------------- 第三篇在本实例上的判词路径 ----------------
def third_path(I, nonF):
    """按《沉积场判定式》v1.0 条文逐级求第三篇可给的判词。nonF：nonf_encoding 对本宿载的结论（True 表示无合 BEAT、LOC 的精确 F 编码）。"""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    end = I.pre[I.t1 + 1]
    allpos = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    steps = []
    steps.append('Y₂：SEP 要求「宿载属 F 类」（〔第三篇〕184）；机制级申报入口要求「律属 F 类」（181）。'
                 + ('本宿载无合 BEAT、LOC 的精确 F 编码（命题 N1、N2），SEP 不能如实申报，Y₂ 判词不取得；同实例乙「反馈律不属 F 类三种数值律，定理六不适用（U9）。故只能用 Y₁」（639）。'
                    if nonF else 'F 编码存在，Y₂ 可用（本实例不出现此支）。'))
    steps.append('若强行以 488 型编码（发生按实际申报、常数律）通过入口：EF4 不成立（485），定理七（二）负判不成立；LOC 响应与编码的空支持冲突，属 E25（554）。其 F 层读数见 F 编码栏。')
    q = {}
    for k in T:
        q[k] = 0 if zk(G, end, k) <= EPS else PROF
    steps.append('Y₁（4.5，241）：q(x) 窗末 s(x) ≤ ε 判 0，否则 ⊥_Prof → ' + str({f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}': q[k] for k in T}))
    steps.append('Y₁（242）：r 判 ⊥_Prof（定理五（二））')
    low = all(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) <= EPS for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2) for k in T)
    ph = 0 if low else PROF
    steps.append(f'Y₁（243）：Part、HDyn 在「W 内 T 各位恒不大于 ε」时判 0，否则 ⊥_Prof → W 内 T 恒 ≤ ε：{low} ⇒ Part = HDyn = {ph}')
    steps.append('Y₁（244）：Act(m)、HistCond(m) 无清单判 ⊥_Undef；（245）Fld 判 ⊥_Prof')
    fld_neg = all(zk(G, end, k) <= EPS for k in allpos) or all(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) <= EPS for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2) for k in allpos)
    steps.append('Y₁ 加申报（247）：q = 1 须「F 类申报与 REC」——F 类申报不可得，q 仍 ⊥_Prof；'
                 f'（248）Fld 退化负判须 Dom5 且窗末无位 > ε 或 W 内一切位恒 ≤ ε：{fld_neg}；'
                 '（249）r 须 PE（196）——信号经教学电流门控界内可塑性，PE 不成立；'
                 '（250）Part 须 AC（198）——输出发放不是纯读取过程（发放写 y 并门控 PLUS），AC 不成立；'
                 '（251）HDyn 须 SAM 与 LOC 且 F 类（引理十，494）——F 类不成立')
    fld = 0 if fld_neg else PROF
    return dict(q=q, r=PROF, Part=ph, HDyn=ph, Fld=fld, steps=steps)

# ---------------- 488 型 F 编码的读数（D10—D19） ----------------
def f_layer(I, positions, ext_entry, eps=EPS):
    """入口拍 t0−1 以申报事件给出 z(t0)；W 内每一作用于位集的可塑性事件按实际时刻编码为常数律机制（发生已申报，cert_eff = 1）。
    ext_entry=True 时 T 位的入口事件记为界外脉冲（E2），否则为 B 内常数律机制（E1）。按 D12、D15—D19、4.2 求值。"""
    G, T, t0, t1 = I.G, I.T, I.t0, I.t1
    s = {k: F(0) for k in positions}
    form, mark = {k: [] for k in positions}, {k: [] for k in positions}
    dom_err = None
    for k in positions:
        z0 = zk(G, I.pre[t0], k)
        if z0 == 0:
            continue
        if z0 < 0:
            dom_err = dom_err or f'入口拍位 w{k[1]}{k[2]} 的 z(t0) = {fr(z0)} < 0，越出 F 定义域（D10、D20）'
            continue
        if ext_entry and k in T:
            if z0 > eps: form[k].append(t0 - 1); mark[k].append(t0 - 1)
        elif z0 > eps:
            form[k].append(t0 - 1)
        s[k] = z0
    devs, atoms = [], []
    for t in range(t0, t1 + 1):
        incr = {k: F(0) for k in positions}
        for e in I.evs[t].values():
            if e['tgt'] in positions and e['id'][0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP'):
                c = e['c']
                act = 1 if abs(c) > eps else 0          # D18 Act_F
                hc = 0                                  # 常数律 Supp_act = ∅（D15）⇒ HistCond_F = 0（D18 后说明）
                atoms.append(dict(id=e['id'], t=t, tgt=e['tgt'], c=c, Act=act, HistCond=hc, Cond=0))
                if act:
                    if s[e['tgt']] <= eps < s[e['tgt']] + c:
                        form[e['tgt']].append(t)
                    incr[e['tgt']] += c                 # 4.2：只入账有效分项
        for k in positions:
            s[k] += incr[k]
            if s[k] < 0 and dom_err is None:
                dom_err = f'拍 {t} 位 w{k[1]}{k[2]} 更新值 {fr(s[k])} < 0（D20）'
        for k in positions:
            if s[k] != zk(G, I.pre[t + 1], k):
                devs.append((t, k, s[k] - zk(G, I.pre[t + 1], k)))
    Src = 1 if any(mark[k] for k in T) else 0
    Ret = 1 if all(s[k] > eps and form[k] for k in T) else 0
    trace = k_and(Src, Ret, 1, 1)
    part = k_or([a['Cond'] for a in atoms])
    hdyn = k_or([k_and(1 if a['tgt'] in T else 0, a['Act'], a['HistCond']) for a in atoms])
    return dict(Src=Src, Ret=Ret, Trace=trace, Part=part, HDyn=hdyn, Q=k_and(trace, part, hdyn),
                atoms=atoms, devs=devs, dom_err=dom_err)
~~~

A.7　lp\_exact.py　与《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 附录 A.5 逐字相同：精确有理线性可行性与证书

~~~python
"""精确有理线性可行性（Fourier–Motzkin，含严格不等式）：可行时给见证点，不可行时给 Motzkin 证书；正文 5.1 定理 L1、引理 L2、L3 的有限自检。
只用标准库。运行：python3 lp_exact.py
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True

def _norm(c):
    return {k: v for k, v in c.items() if v != 0}

def check_point(cons, x):
    for coef, const, strict in cons:
        val = sum(v * x.get(k, F(0)) for k, v in coef.items()) + const
        if (strict and not val < 0) or (not strict and not val <= 0):
            return False
    return True

def check_cert(cons, lam):
    if any(l < 0 for l in lam.values()):
        return False
    tot = {}
    s = F(0)
    strict_pos = False
    for i, l in lam.items():
        if l == 0:
            continue
        coef, const, strict = cons[i]
        for k, v in coef.items():
            tot[k] = tot.get(k, F(0)) + l * v
        s += l * const
        strict_pos = strict_pos or strict
    if any(v != 0 for v in tot.values()):
        return False
    return s > 0 or (s == 0 and strict_pos)

def feasible(cons):
    """Fourier–Motzkin。每条导出约束携带其关于原约束的非负乘子。"""
    rows = []
    for i, (coef, const, strict) in enumerate(cons):
        rows.append((_norm(dict(coef)), F(const), bool(strict), {i: F(1)}))
    order = []
    for r in rows:
        for k in r[0]:
            if k not in order:
                order.append(k)
    stages = []                  # 每次消元前的行集，用于回代
    for var in order:
        stages.append((var, rows))
        pos, neg, zero = [], [], []
        for r in rows:
            a = r[0].get(var, F(0))
            (pos if a > 0 else neg if a < 0 else zero).append(r)
        new = list(zero)
        seen = set()
        for rp in pos:
            ap = rp[0][var]
            for rn in neg:
                an = -rn[0][var]
                # an·rp + ap·rn 消去 var
                coef = {}
                for k, v in rp[0].items():
                    coef[k] = coef.get(k, F(0)) + an * v
                for k, v in rn[0].items():
                    coef[k] = coef.get(k, F(0)) + ap * v
                coef = _norm(coef)
                coef.pop(var, None)
                const = an * rp[1] + ap * rn[1]
                strict = rp[2] or rn[2]
                lam = {}
                for i, l in rp[3].items():
                    lam[i] = lam.get(i, F(0)) + an * l
                for i, l in rn[3].items():
                    lam[i] = lam.get(i, F(0)) + ap * l
                key = (tuple(sorted(coef.items())), const, strict)
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                new.append((coef, const, strict, lam))
        rows = new
        for r in rows:          # 早停：出现常数矛盾
            if not r[0] and (r[1] > 0 or (r[1] == 0 and r[2])):
                return False, r[3]
    for r in rows:
        if r[1] > 0 or (r[1] == 0 and r[2]):
            return False, r[3]
    # 回代求见证点
    x = {}
    for var, rws in reversed(stages):
        lo, lo_s, hi, hi_s = None, False, None, False
        for coef, const, strict, _ in rws:
            a = coef.get(var, F(0))
            if a == 0:
                continue
            rest = sum(v * x[k] for k, v in coef.items() if k != var) + const
            bnd = -rest / a          # a·var + rest (<,≤) 0
            if a > 0:                # var (<,≤) bnd
                if hi is None or bnd < hi or (bnd == hi and strict):
                    hi, hi_s = bnd, strict if (hi is None or bnd < hi) else (hi_s or strict)
            else:                    # var (>,≥) bnd
                if lo is None or bnd > lo or (bnd == lo and strict):
                    lo, lo_s = bnd, strict if (lo is None or bnd > lo) else (lo_s or strict)
        if lo is None and hi is None:
            val = F(0)
        elif lo is None:
            val = hi - 1 if hi_s else hi
        elif hi is None:
            val = lo + 1 if lo_s else lo
        else:
            val = (lo + hi) / 2 if (lo_s or hi_s) else lo
        x[var] = val
    return True, x

def selftest(n=4000, seed=20261102):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    st = dict(feas=0, infeas=0, bad=0)
    for _ in range(n):
        d = rng.randint(1, 4)
        vs = [f'x{i}' for i in range(d)]
        m = rng.randint(1, 9)
        cons = []
        for _m in range(m):
            coef = {v: F(rng.randint(-4, 4), rng.randint(1, 3)) for v in vs if rng.random() < 0.8}
            cons.append((coef, F(rng.randint(-6, 6), rng.randint(1, 4)), rng.random() < 0.5))
        ok, obj = feasible(cons)
        if ok:
            st['feas'] += 1
            st['bad'] += not check_point(cons, obj)
        else:
            st['infeas'] += 1
            st['bad'] += not check_cert(cons, obj)
    # 边界例：x ≤ 0 且 x ≥ 0 可行；x < 0 且 x ≥ 0 不可行；x < 1 且 x > 0 可行（取中点）
    edge = [([({'x': F(1)}, F(0), False), ({'x': F(-1)}, F(0), False)], True),
            ([({'x': F(1)}, F(0), True), ({'x': F(-1)}, F(0), False)], False),
            ([({'x': F(1)}, F(-1), True), ({'x': F(-1)}, F(0), True)], True),
            ([({}, F(0), True)], False), ([({}, F(0), False)], True)]
    for cons, exp in edge:
        ok, obj = feasible(cons)
        st['bad'] += (ok != exp) or (ok and not check_point(cons, obj)) or ((not ok) and not check_cert(cons, obj))
    return st

if __name__ == '__main__':
    st = selftest()
    print('精确有理 Fourier–Motzkin 自检（随机 4000 组，维数 1—4，严格与非严格混合；另 5 个边界例）')
    print(f'  可行 {st["feas"]}（见证点逐条代入核验），不可行 {st["infeas"]}（Motzkin 证书独立核验），核验失败 {st["bad"]}')
~~~

A.8　decision.py　与《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 附录 A.6 逐字相同：定理 D1、D2 的判定器

~~~python
"""门控类 R 层判定器（正文 5.2、5.3 与第六节）：𝒟₁ 上函数依赖的精确判定（定理 D1）、Part 与 HDyn 判词与三档负向（定理 D2）、T_max 覆盖（定理 TM）。
声明的干预域：𝒟₁（单拍联合 LOC，σ_k ∈ [b_k, hi_k]）；𝒟_m（多拍）只有引理 C、C′ 的充分负判。
夹限个别化：饱和的位—拍按「分列」与「联合（D9）」两读法各求，一致则取之，否则 ⊥_Ind。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。运行：python3 decision.py（输出含「用时」行，随机器变化，其余确定）
"""
import sys, random, itertools, time
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
import lp_exact as LP
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr
# ---------- 仿射式：(常数, {变量: 系数}) ----------

def A0(c):
    return (F(c), {})

def avar(name):
    return (F(0), {name: F(1)})

def aadd(*xs):
    c, d = F(0), {}
    for x0, x1 in xs:
        c += x0
        for k, v in x1.items():
            d[k] = d.get(k, F(0)) + v
    return (c, {k: v for k, v in d.items() if v != 0})

def ascale(a, s):
    return (a[0] * s, {k: v * s for k, v in a[1].items() if v * s != 0})

def is_const(a):
    return not a[1]

def le0(a):      # a ≤ 0
    return (dict(a[1]), a[0], False)

def lt0(a):      # a < 0
    return (dict(a[1]), a[0], True)

class Stats:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lp = 0
        self.nodes = 0

ST = Stats()

def feas(R):
    ST.lp += 1
    return LP.feasible(R)

def simplify(R):
    """同方向约束只留最紧者（系数向量按最大绝对值归一）。等价变形，不改变可行集（正文引理 L3）。"""
    best = {}
    for coef, const, strict in R:
        m = max(abs(v) for v in coef.values())
        key = tuple(sorted((k, v / m) for k, v in coef.items()))
        c = const / m
        old = best.get(key)
        if old is None or c > old[0] or (c == old[0] and strict and not old[1]):
            best[key] = (c, strict)
    return [(dict(k), c, s_) for k, (c, s_) in best.items()]

def split(R, conds):
    """conds：[(标签, [约束])]；返回可行的 (标签, R + 约束)。常值约束直接求值。"""
    out = []
    for lab, cs in conds:
        add, ok = [], True
        for c in cs:
            if not c[0]:
                v = c[1]
                if (c[2] and not v < 0) or (not c[2] and not v <= 0):
                    ok = False
                    break
            else:
                add.append(c)
        if not ok:
            continue
        R2 = simplify(R + add) if add else R
        if add:
            f, _ = feas(R2)
            if not f:
                continue
        out.append((lab, R2))
    return out

def sym_step(G, st, R, sig, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """定义 2 的符号一拍。st = (w{(i,j): 仿射}, v[仿射], x[常], y[常])。返回 [(R′, st′, 事件{id: 仿射 c}, 净增量{(i,j): 仿射})]。"""
    w, v, x, y = st
    name, I = sig
    s = G.pat[name]
    vt = [aadd(ascale(v[j], G.lam[j]), *[w[(i, j)] for i in range(G.n_in) if s[i]], A0(I[j])) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    gate_opts = [[]]
    regs = [R]
    combos = [([], R)]
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        nxt = []
        for gs, Rg in combos:
            d = aadd(vt[j], A0(-G.theta[j]))      # vt − θ
            for lab, R2 in split(Rg, [(1, [le0(ascale(d, -1))]), (0, [lt0(d)])]):
                nxt.append((gs + [lab], R2))
        combos = nxt
    out = []
    for g, Rg in combos:
        v2 = [A0(G.vreset[j]) if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
        x2 = [G.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)]
        y2 = [G.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
        ev0 = {}
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            ev0[('LEAK', (j,))] = ascale(v[j], G.lam[j] - 1)
            for i in range(G.n_in):
                if s[i]:
                    ev0[('SYN', (i, j))] = w[(i, j)]
            if I[j] != 0:
                ev0[('INJ', (j,))] = A0(I[j])
            if g[j]:
                ev0[('RESET', (j,))] = aadd(A0(G.vreset[j]), ascale(vt[j], -1))
                ev0[('YIN', (j,))] = A0(1)
            ev0[('YDEC', (j,))] = A0((G.dy[j] - 1) * y[j])
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            ev0[('XDEC', (i,))] = A0((G.dx[i] - 1) * x[i])
            if s[i]:
                ev0[('XIN', (i,))] = A0(1)
        Ssum = {}
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                parts = [w[(i, j)]]
                if plastic and bap and g[j]:
                    c = A0(G.Ap * x2[i]); ev0[('PLUS', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                if plastic and bap and s[i]:
                    c = A0(-G.Am * y2[j]); ev0[('MINUS', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                if plastic and G.rho != 0:
                    c = ascale(aadd(w[(i, j)], A0(-G.b[i][j])), -G.rho); ev0[('RELAX', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                Ssum[(i, j)] = aadd(*parts)
        pieces = [({}, Rg)]
        for k, S in Ssum.items():
            lo, hi = G.lo[k[0]][k[1]], G.hi[k[0]][k[1]]
            nxt = []
            for asg, Rp in pieces:
                opts = [('lo', [lt0(aadd(S, A0(-lo)))]),                                       # S < lo
                        ('mid', [le0(aadd(A0(lo), ascale(S, -1))), le0(aadd(S, A0(-hi)))]),     # lo ≤ S ≤ hi
                        ('hi', [lt0(aadd(A0(hi), ascale(S, -1)))])]                              # S > hi
                for lab, R2 in split(Rp, opts):
                    a2 = dict(asg); a2[k] = lab
                    nxt.append((a2, R2))
            pieces = nxt
        for asg, Rp in pieces:
            ev = dict(ev0)
            w2, net = {}, {}
            for k, S in Ssum.items():
                lab = asg[k]
                lo, hi = G.lo[k[0]][k[1]], G.hi[k[0]][k[1]]
                if lab == 'mid':
                    w2[k] = S
                else:
                    b = A0(lo if lab == 'lo' else hi)
                    w2[k] = b
                    ev[('CLIP', k)] = aadd(b, ascale(S, -1))
                net[k] = aadd(w2[k], ascale(w[k], -1))
            out.append((Rp, (w2, v2, x2, y2), ev, net))
    return out

def point_of(R, names):
    ok, x = feas(R)
    assert ok
    return {n: x.get(n, F(0)) for n in names}

def differs(R, a, c0):
    """R 上是否存在点使仿射 a ≠ c0；返回见证点或 None。"""
    if is_const(a):
        if a[0] != c0:
            ok, x = feas(R) if R else (True, {})
            return x
        return None
    for cons in (lt0(aadd(a, A0(-c0))), lt0(aadd(A0(c0), ascale(a, -1)))):
        ok, x = feas(simplify(R + [cons]))
        if ok:
            return x
    return None

# ---------- 实例与精确判定 ----------
class Decider:
    def __init__(self, I, budget=200000):
        self.I, self.G = I, I.G
        self.budget = budget
        self.sat = {}            # (k, t) 饱和的 T 位—拍 → 实际净增量
        for t in range(len(I.hist)):
            for e in I.evs[t].values():
                if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP' and e['tgt'] in I.T:
                    k = e['tgt']
                    self.sat[(k, t)] = I.pre[t + 1][0][k[1]][k[2]] - I.pre[t][0][k[1]][k[2]]
        self.fd1 = {}            # 事件 id → (u, 见证 dict) ；只记判 1 者
        self.fd1_net = {}
        self.complete_u = {}     # u → 是否在预算内穷尽
        self.verify_fail = 0
        self._run()
    def avail_T(self, u):
        return [k for k in self.I.T if self.I.is_avail(k, u)]
    def targets(self):
        I = self.I
        return {e['id'] for e in I.Wevents()}
    def _run(self):
        I, G = self.I, self.G
        tgt = self.targets()
        us = [u for u in range(0, I.t1 + 1) if self.avail_T(u)]
        for u in us:
            S = self.avail_T(u)
            names = [('s',) + k for k in S]
            w0 = {(i, j): A0(I.pre[u][0][i][j]) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)}
            R0 = []
            for k, nm in zip(S, names):
                w0[(k[1], k[2])] = avar(nm)
                R0.append(le0(aadd(A0(G.b[k[1]][k[2]]), ascale(avar(nm), -1))))      # b − σ ≤ 0
                R0.append(le0(aadd(avar(nm), A0(-G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]))))               # σ − hi ≤ 0
            st0 = (w0, [A0(c) for c in I.pre[u][1]], list(I.pre[u][2]), list(I.pre[u][3]))
            nodes = 0
            stack = [(u, st0, R0)]
            complete = True
            while stack:
                t, st, R = stack.pop()
                nodes += 1
                if nodes > self.budget:
                    complete = False
                    break
                for R2, st2, ev, net in sym_step(G, st, R, I.hist[t], **I.kw):
                    for eid_s, e in I.evs[t].items():
                        if eid_s not in tgt or eid_s in self.fd1:
                            continue
                        key = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
                        if key not in ev:
                            wit = point_of(R2, names)
                        else:
                            x = differs(R2, ev[key], e['c'])
                            wit = None if x is None else {n: x.get(n, F(0)) for n in names}
                        if wit is not None:
                            self._verify(u, S, names, wit, e)
                            self.fd1[eid_s] = (u, {k: wit[nm] for k, nm in zip(S, names)})
                    for (k, tt), net0 in self.sat.items():
                        if tt == t and (k, t) not in self.fd1_net and t >= I.t0:
                            x = differs(R2, net[(k[1], k[2])], net0)
                            if x is not None:
                                self.fd1_net[(k, t)] = (u, {kk: x.get(nm, F(0)) for kk, nm in zip(S, names)})
                    if t + 1 <= I.t1:
                        stack.append((t + 1, st2, R2))
            ST.nodes += nodes
            self.complete_u[u] = complete
    def _verify(self, u, S, names, wit, e):
        I, G = self.I, self.G
        assign = {k: wit[nm] for k, nm in zip(S, names)}
        pre, evs, _ = GK.run_with(G, GK.setw(I.pre[u], assign), I.hist[u:e['t'] + 1], t_start=u, **I.kw)
        if GK.same_event(evs, e):
            self.verify_fail += 1
    def fd1_value(self, e):
        if e['id'] in self.fd1:
            return 1
        us = [u for u in self.complete_u if u <= e['t']]
        return 0 if all(self.complete_u[u] for u in us) else GK.COV

def path_act(I, sources, e):
    """实际事件图（D13，只含实际发生的事件；读边、写边、结构位持存边）上自 sources = {(结点, 拍)} 是否可达 e。"""
    if not sources:
        return 0
    reach = set()
    t0 = min(u for _, u in sources)
    for t in range(t0, e['t'] + 1):
        reach |= {k for (k, u) in sources if u == t}
        if t == e['t']:
            return 1 if (e['reads'] & reach) else 0
        nxt = {k for k in reach if k[0] == 'w'}
        for ev in I.evs[t].values():
            if ev['reads'] & reach:
                nxt.add(ev['tgt'])
        reach = nxt
    return 0

def eta_exact(I, D, e, bfd=True, domain='D1'):
    """返回 dict：值、档位、依据。domain='D1' 用 𝒟₁ 精确判定；'Dm' 另用引理 C、C′ 与钉位见证。"""
    if GK.path(I, e) == 0:
        return dict(v=0, tier='a', why='Path_slot = 0（R3）', fd=0)
    fd = D.fd1_value(e)
    wit = D.fd1.get(e['id'])
    if domain == 'Dm' and fd != 1:
        dv, why = GK.dep(I, e)
        if dv == 1:
            fd, wit = 1, None
        elif dv == 0 and fd == 0:
            fd = 0
        else:
            fd = GK.COV
    if fd == 1:
        if wit is not None:
            u, asg = wit
            src = {(k, u) for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
            why = f'𝒟₁ 见证 拍{u} ' + ','.join(f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}←{fr(val)}' for k, val in asg.items())
        else:
            src = {(k, u) for k in I.T for u in I.avail[k] if u <= e['t']}
            why = '钉位见证（𝒟_m）'
        if path_act(I, src, e):
            return dict(v=1, tier='+', why=why + '；实际图可达', fd=1)
        return dict(v='⊥_Open', tier='+?', why=why + '；实际图不可达（依赖只经未发生支路）', fd=1)
    if fd == 0:
        return dict(v=0 if bfd else '⊥_Over', tier='b', why=f'FD_{domain} = 0（全部分支不可行或常值相等）' + ('' if bfd else '；未申报 B_FD'), fd=0)
    return dict(v=GK.COV, tier='c', why='预算内未穷尽', fd=GK.COV)

def judge(I, D, bfd=True, domain='D1'):
    """Part、HDyn 判词（分列读法与联合读法；二者一致取之，否则 ⊥_Ind）。"""
    T = I.T
    rows = []
    hd_sep, hd_joint, pt_sep, pt_joint = [], [], [], []
    satk = {(k, t) for (k, t) in D.sat if t >= I.t0}
    for e in I.Wevents():
        ex = eta_exact(I, D, e, bfd, domain)
        a = 1 if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0
        ko = GK.ko_act(I.G, I.pre[e['t']], I.evs[e['t']], e['id'])[0]
        rows.append(dict(e=e, a=a, ko=ko, **ex))
        if e['tgt'] in T:
            hd_sep.append(GK.k_and(a, ex['v']))
            if (e['tgt'], e['t']) not in satk:
                hd_joint.append(GK.k_and(a, ex['v']))
        if e['car'] == RW.B:
            pt_sep.append(ex['v'])
            if not (e['tgt'] in T and (e['tgt'], e['t']) in satk):
                pt_joint.append(ex['v'])
    for (k, t) in satk:      # 联合事件：c = 净增量，读取 = 诸成员之并
        net0 = D.sat[(k, t)]
        mem = [e for e in I.evs[t].values() if e['tgt'] == k]
        reads = frozenset().union(*[e['reads'] for e in mem])
        pseudo = dict(id=('NET', (k[1], k[2]), t), t=t, reads=reads, tgt=k, car=RW.B, c=net0)
        if net0 == 0:
            a = '⊥_Joint' if any(e['c'] != 0 for e in mem) else 0
        else:
            a = 1 if abs(net0) > EPS else 0
        if GK.path(I, pseudo) == 0:
            ev = 0
        elif (k, t) in D.fd1_net:
            u, asg = D.fd1_net[(k, t)]
            src = {(kk, u) for kk, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][kk[1]][kk[2]]}
            ev = 1 if path_act(I, src, pseudo) else '⊥_Open'
        else:
            ev = 0 if bfd else '⊥_Over'
        hd_joint.append(GK.k_and(a, ev)); pt_joint.append(ev)
    hs, hj, ps, pj = GK.k_or(hd_sep), GK.k_or(hd_joint), GK.k_or(pt_sep), GK.k_or(pt_joint)
    H = hs if hs == hj else '⊥_Ind'
    P = ps if ps == pj else '⊥_Ind'
    return dict(rows=rows, HDyn=H, Part=P, HDyn_sep=hs, HDyn_joint=hj, nsat=len(satk))

def premises(I, D, res, bfd):
    """判 0 的前件清单（EF1—EF5 的对应物，正文 5.3）。"""
    base = ['MOD-R（R1：事件清单完备，模型结构）', 'R2/R3（读取集与槽位图闭合，模型结构）', 'REC', 'ENT', 'POS', 'BEAT-R（模型拍即 L 拍、ε 申报）']
    tiers = {r['tier'] for r in res['rows'] if r['v'] == 0}
    if 'b' in tiers:
        base += ['DOM[𝒟₁]（干预域事前申报）', 'B_FD[𝒟₁]（函数依赖读法的对象层对应）']
    if res['nsat']:
        base += ['IND_CLIP（夹限个别化，两读法一致才不需要）']
    return base

# ---------- 报告 ----------
def show(I, title, bfd=True, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.time()
    D = Decider(I)
    res = judge(I, D, bfd)
    resn = judge(I, D, False)
    print(f'【{title}】{I.name}')
    print(f'  W = 拍 {I.t0}—{I.t1}；T 首个可用拍 {min(min(I.avail[k]) for k in I.T if I.avail[k])}；'
          f'𝒟₁ 起点拍数 {len(D.complete_u)}，均在预算内穷尽：{all(D.complete_u.values())}；见证具体重跑核验失败 {D.verify_fail}；饱和位—拍 {len(D.sat)}')
    if verbose:
        print('  作用对象属 T 的 W 内事件：事件@拍 | c | a（读出/KO） | η | 档位 | 依据')
        for r in res['rows']:
            e = r['e']
            if e['tgt'] in I.T:
                print(f'    {e["id"][0]}{e["id"][1]}@{e["t"]} | {fr(e["c"])} | {r["a"]}/{r["ko"]} | {r["v"]} | {r["tier"]} | {r["why"]}')
    cnt = {}
    for r in res['rows']:
        if r['e']['car'] == RW.B:
            cnt[(r['tier'], str(r['v']))] = cnt.get((r['tier'], str(r['v'])), 0) + 1
    print('  B 内事件 κ 的档位分布：', '；'.join(f'{t}:{v}×{n}' for (t, v), n in sorted(cnt.items())))
    print(f'  判词（申报 B_FD[𝒟₁]）：Part = {res["Part"]}，HDyn = {res["HDyn"]}（分列 {res["HDyn_sep"]}／联合 {res["HDyn_joint"]}）')
    print(f'  判词（不申报 B_FD）：Part = {resn["Part"]}，HDyn = {resn["HDyn"]}')
    if res['HDyn'] == 0 or res['Part'] == 0:
        print('  判 0 的前件清单：', '；'.join(premises(I, D, res, bfd)))
    print(f'  （用时 {time.time() - t0:.1f} 秒）')
    return D, res, resn

def random_validation(n_inst=40, seed=7, samples=60):
    """定理 D1 的有限核验：随机 K_g 成员与史；对每一判 FD₁ = 0 的事件，在 𝒟₁ 内随机抽样设定，均不改变该事件；
    对每一判 1 者见证已具体重跑。另与 gate_criteria 的有限设定判据对照（后者判 1 ⇒ 此处判 1）。"""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    st = dict(inst=0, events=0, fd0=0, fd1=0, samp=0, viol=0, vfail=0, gk1_not1=0)
    for _ in range(n_inst):
        p = GC.snn_params(S)
        p['lam'] = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)] * 2
        p['Ap'] = F(rng.randint(1, 4), 16); p['Am'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16)
        p['b'] = [[F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        p['hi'] = [[F(rng.choice([1, 2, 3]), 2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        G = GC.Gate(rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10)]), **p)
        tv = [F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)]
        form = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE] * rng.randint(1, 3)
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), 0)) for _ in range(rng.randint(3, 7))]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, form + win, len(form))
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in I.T):
            continue
        D = Decider(I, budget=50000)
        st['inst'] += 1; st['vfail'] += D.verify_fail
        for e in I.Wevents():
            v = D.fd1_value(e)
            st['events'] += 1
            if v == 1:
                st['fd1'] += 1
            elif v == 0:
                st['fd0'] += 1
                us = [u for u in D.complete_u if u <= e['t']]
                for _s in range(samples // 10 if us else 0):
                    u = rng.choice(us)
                    asg = {k: G.b[k[1]][k[2]] + F(rng.randint(0, 48), 48) * (G.hi[k[1]][k[2]] - G.b[k[1]][k[2]]) for k in D.avail_T(u)}
                    _, evs, _ = GK.run_with(G, GK.setw(I.pre[u], asg), I.hist[u:e['t'] + 1], t_start=u, **I.kw)
                    st['samp'] += 1
                    if not GK.same_event(evs, e):
                        st['viol'] += 1
            dv, _ = GK.dep(I, e)
            if dv == 1 and v != 1:
                # gate_criteria 的钉位见证属 𝒟_m，可在 𝒟₁ 之外；只统计其单拍见证不被 𝒟₁ 判 1 的情形
                runs, _, _ = I.cf()
                single = any((not r['lab'].startswith('钉位')) and r['u'] <= e['t'] and not GK.same_event(r['evs'], e) for r in runs)
                st['gk1_not1'] += single
    return st

def tmax_coverage():
    """T_max 归约的判词覆盖（正文定理 TM）：形成史使 4 个入口突触都成迹应，T_max = 4 位；
    对 T_max 的一切非空子集求 Part、HDyn，核单调性（T ⊆ T′ ⇒ 判词按真值序不减，且 1、0 不翻转）。"""
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    hist = [('AB', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0)), NONE, ('B', (F(1, 2), 0)), NONE, ('A', (F(1, 4), 0))]
    allT = [('w', i, 0) for i in range(4)]
    base = GK.Instance('', G, hist, 3, T=allT)
    end = base.pre[len(hist)]
    Tmax = [k for k in allT if GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and base.form[k]]
    print(f'  史 (AB+教学 1, 静, 静, A, 静, B+教学 1/2, 静, A+教学 1/4)，W = 拍 3—7；T_max = {[f"w{k[1]}{k[2]}" for k in Tmax]}')
    res = {}
    for r in range(1, len(Tmax) + 1):
        for Ts in itertools.combinations(Tmax, r):
            I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, 3, T=list(Ts))
            D = Decider(I, budget=50000)
            j = judge(I, D, True)
            res[Ts] = (j['Part'], j['HDyn'], all(D.complete_u.values()), D.verify_fail)
    order = {0: 0, '⊥': 1, 1: 2}
    viol = 0
    for Ta in res:
        for Tb in res:
            if set(Ta) < set(Tb):
                for idx in (0, 1):
                    va, vb = res[Ta][idx], res[Tb][idx]
                    if va in (0, 1) and vb in (0, 1) and va > vb:
                        viol += 1
                    if va == 1 and vb != 1:
                        viol += 1
                    if vb == 0 and va != 0:
                        viol += 1
    for Ts, v in sorted(res.items(), key=lambda kv: len(kv[0])):
        print(f'    T = {{{",".join(f"w{k[1]}{k[2]}" for k in Ts)}}}：Part = {v[0]}，HDyn = {v[1]}，穷尽 {v[2]}，见证核验失败 {v[3]}')
    print(f'  子集对 T ⊂ T′ 上单调性违例 {viol}（1 在扩大时保持为 1，0 在缩小时保持为 0）')
    return viol

def main():
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    P = GK.Instance('s1 冻结申报史', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    N = GK.Instance('(A+教学 1, B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    O1 = GK.Instance('s1 申报史，W = (A+教学 1, 静, 静)', G, FORM + REST + [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE], 26)
    FORM1 = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE]
    O2 = GK.Instance('形成 1 次；入口拍 A+教学；W = (B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G,
                     FORM1 + [NONE] * 7 + [('A', (F(1), 0))] + [('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 11)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 4); S.W0 = F(1, 2)
    Gs = GC.snn_gate(S)
    SAT = GK.Instance('W0 = 1/2、A₊ = 1/4；形成 (A+教学, 静, 静)×6，静置 8，W 为 (A,静,静,B,静,静)×3', Gs,
                      FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 8); S.W0 = F(1, 4)
    print('一、实例判词（𝒟₁ 精确判定；HistCond 三档负向；分列/联合两读法）')
    for I, t in ((P, '实例 P'), (N, '实例 N'), (O1, '实例 O1'), (O2, '实例 O2'), (SAT, '实例 SAT（夹限生效）')):
        show(I, t, verbose=(I is not SAT))
        print()
    print('二、定理 D1 的有限核验（随机 K_g 成员与史；FD₁ = 0 的事件在 𝒟₁ 内随机抽样均不变；判 1 的见证已具体重跑）')
    st = random_validation()
    print(f'  实例 {st["inst"]}，W 内事件 {st["events"]}：FD₁ = 1 者 {st["fd1"]}、= 0 者 {st["fd0"]}；抽样 {st["samp"]} 次，违例 {st["viol"]}；'
          f'见证重跑失败 {st["vfail"]}；有限设定判据的单拍见证未被 𝒟₁ 判 1 者 {st["gk1_not1"]}')
    print('三、T_max 归约的判词覆盖')
    tmax_coverage()
    print(f'四、线性可行性调用合计 {ST.lp} 次；符号分支结点合计 {ST.nodes}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.9　t13\_gate.py　与《物质场具化判定式》v1.0 附录 A.4 逐字相同：多拍门控库（𝒦\_g、𝒦\_PA^δ、𝒦\_PA^≺ 的运行与事件）

~~~python
"""《物质场具化判定式》附录程序：多拍门控通道。精确有理运算，只用标准库。
对象（正文 3.6、5.7）：
  mode = 'g'      《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 定义 1—2 的 𝒦_g（gate_of[j] = j），及第三因子变体（gate_of[j] ≠ j，𝒦_PA^3）；
  mode = 'delay'  𝒦_PA^δ：门控单元的发放以权重 U[k][j] 延迟一拍输入单元 j 的积分（状态加辅助位 h = 上一拍发放向量）；
  mode = 'dag'    𝒦_PA^≺：同拍发放输入，U 的支撑图须为有向无环图，按申报拓扑次序 order 逐单元积分、门控。
  同拍循环发放输入（同时方程）不属本篇工作域：simultaneous_solutions 只用于展示其解可不唯一（正文 3.6 例 3.6-1）。
函数：
  validate          类成员检查（参数域、DAG 次序）
  step / run        一拍与多拍运行（返回逐拍拍前状态、门控、门控输入、全部事件 (发生, 分项)）
  r1_check          引理 R1′（K_t 闭合：拍前值 + 指向该结点的分项和 = 拍后值）
  simultaneous_solutions  同拍方程组 g_j = [base_j + Σ_k U[k][j] g_k ≥ θ_j] 的全部解（枚举）
  prop_bounds       命题 M-1 的传播递推（权重差系数 1 − ρ·[plastic]；delay 含 h 项）
  event_bound       各事件种类在拍 t_e 的分项差上界 B_e（含 RESET 等全部种类）
  m1_certificate    多拍稳健证书 M-1（发生型规则只用于发生由门控、字母、开关决定的种类；CLIP 与受 h 副作用的 FIN 用分项规则）
  beta_star         单位副作用剖面下裕度条件可容许的最大缩放因子（干预界）
  m2_twin           命题 M-2：全局固定 pat_B 的 H0 孪生构造
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product
ZERO = F(0)
KINDS_W = ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP', 'SYN')

def clip(a, lo, hi):
    return min(hi, max(lo, a))

def _dx(P, i):
    d = P['dx']
    return d[i] if isinstance(d, list) else d

def _dy(P, j):
    d = P['dy']
    return d[j] if isinstance(d, list) else d

def validate(P, nin, nout):
    """类成员检查。返回 (True, '') 或 (False, 原因)。"""
    for j in range(nout):
        if not (0 <= P['lam'][j] <= 1):
            return False, 'lam'
        if not (0 <= _dy(P, j) <= 1):
            return False, 'dy'
    for i in range(nin):
        if not (0 <= _dx(P, i) <= 1):
            return False, 'dx'
    if not (0 <= P['rho'] <= 1) or P['Ap'] < 0 or P['Am'] < 0:
        return False, 'rho/A'
    mode = P.get('mode', 'g')
    U = P.get('U')
    if mode == 'g':
        if U is not None and any(U[k][j] != 0 for k in range(nout) for j in range(nout)):
            return False, 'U in K_g'
    elif mode == 'dag':
        pos = {j: n for n, j in enumerate(P['order'])}
        if sorted(P['order']) != list(range(nout)):
            return False, 'order'
        for k in range(nout):
            for j in range(nout):
                if U[k][j] != 0 and not pos[k] < pos[j]:
                    return False, 'not DAG under order'
    elif mode != 'delay':
        return False, 'mode'
    return True, ''

def _base(P, st, s, I):
    w, v, x, y, h = st
    nin, nout = len(x), len(v)
    return [P['lam'][j] * v[j] + sum((w[i][j] for i in range(nin) if s[i]), ZERO) + I[j] for j in range(nout)]

def step(P, st, s, I):
    """st = (w, v, x, y, h)。返回 (新态, g, vt, ev)；ev[事件] = (发生, 分项)，不发生时分项记 0。"""
    w, v, x, y, h = st
    nin, nout = len(x), len(v)
    mode = P.get('mode', 'g')
    U = P.get('U') or [[ZERO] * nout for _ in range(nout)]
    base = _base(P, st, s, I)
    if mode == 'g':
        vt = base[:]
        g = [vt[j] >= P['theta'][j] for j in range(nout)]
    elif mode == 'delay':
        vt = [base[j] + sum((U[k][j] for k in range(nout) if h[k]), ZERO) for j in range(nout)]
        g = [vt[j] >= P['theta'][j] for j in range(nout)]
    else:  # dag：按申报拓扑次序
        vt = [None] * nout
        g = [None] * nout
        for j in P['order']:
            vt[j] = base[j] + sum((U[k][j] for k in range(nout) if U[k][j] != 0 and g[k]), ZERO)
            g[j] = vt[j] >= P['theta'][j]
    v2 = [P['vres'][j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(nout)]
    x2 = [_dx(P, i) * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(nin)]
    y2 = [_dy(P, j) * y[j] + (1 if g[j] else 0) for j in range(nout)]
    h2 = [1 if g[j] else 0 for j in range(nout)]
    w2 = [[None] * nout for _ in range(nin)]
    ev = {}
    pl, bp = P['plastic'], P['bap']
    for i in range(nin):
        for j in range(nout):
            G = g[P['gate_of'][j]]
            occp = bool(pl and bp and G)
            occm = bool(pl and bp and s[i])
            pp = P['Ap'] * x2[i] if occp else ZERO
            pm = -P['Am'] * y2[P['gate_of'][j]] if occm else ZERO
            occr = bool(pl and P['rho'] != 0)
            pr = -P['rho'] * (w[i][j] - P['b'][i][j]) if pl else ZERO
            S = w[i][j] + pp + pm + pr
            w2[i][j] = clip(S, P['lo'], P['hi'])
            cc = w2[i][j] - S
            ev[('PLUS', i, j)] = (occp, pp)
            ev[('MINUS', i, j)] = (occm, pm)
            ev[('RELAX', i, j)] = (occr, pr)
            ev[('CLIP', i, j)] = (cc != 0, cc)
            ev[('SYN', i, j)] = (bool(s[i]), w[i][j] if s[i] else ZERO)
    for i in range(nin):
        ev[('XIN', i)] = (bool(s[i]), F(1) if s[i] else ZERO)
        ev[('XDEC', i)] = (True, (_dx(P, i) - 1) * x[i])
    for j in range(nout):
        ev[('LEAK', j)] = (True, (P['lam'][j] - 1) * v[j])
        ev[('INJ', j)] = (I[j] != 0, I[j])
        ev[('RESET', j)] = (g[j], P['vres'][j] - vt[j] if g[j] else ZERO)
        ev[('YIN', j)] = (g[j], F(1) if g[j] else ZERO)
        ev[('YDEC', j)] = (True, (_dy(P, j) - 1) * y[j])
        for k in range(nout):
            if U[k][j] != 0 and mode != 'g':
                on = bool(h[k]) if mode == 'delay' else bool(g[k])
                ev[('FIN', k, j)] = (on, U[k][j] if on else ZERO)
        if mode == 'delay':
            ev[('HCOPY', j)] = (True, F(h2[j] - h[j]))
    return (w2, v2, x2, y2, h2), g, vt, ev

def copy_state(st):
    w, v, x, y, h = st
    return ([row[:] for row in w], v[:], x[:], y[:], h[:])

def run(P, st0, seq, u=None, setting=None, side=None):
    """seq：[(s, I)]。拍 u 开始前把 setting（{(i, j): 值}）与 side（{('w',i,j)|('v',j)|('x',i)|('y',j): 增量；('h',k): 翻转}）施于状态。
    返回逐拍记录 dict(pre=拍前态, g, vt, ev)。"""
    st = copy_state(st0)
    out = []
    for t, (s, I) in enumerate(seq):
        if u is not None and t == u:
            w, v, x, y, h = copy_state(st)
            for (i, j), val in (setting or {}).items():
                w[i][j] = val
            for key, d in (side or {}).items():
                if key[0] == 'w':
                    w[key[1]][key[2]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'v':
                    v[key[1]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'x':
                    x[key[1]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'y':
                    y[key[1]] += d
                elif key[0] == 'h':
                    if d:
                        h[key[1]] = 1 - h[key[1]]
            st = (w, v, x, y, h)
        pre = copy_state(st)
        st, g, vt, ev = step(P, st, s, I)
        out.append({'pre': pre, 'g': g, 'vt': vt, 'ev': ev, 'post': copy_state(st)})
    return out

def r1_check(P, rec):
    """引理 R1′：对一拍记录逐结点核对 拍前值 + Σ 分项 = 拍后值。返回不一致数。"""
    w, v, x, y, h = rec['pre']
    w2, v2, x2, y2, h2 = rec['post']
    ev = rec['ev']
    nin, nout = len(x), len(v)
    bad = 0
    for i in range(nin):
        for j in range(nout):
            tot = sum(ev[(k, i, j)][1] for k in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP'))
            bad += (w[i][j] + tot != w2[i][j])
        bad += (x[i] + ev[('XDEC', i)][1] + ev[('XIN', i)][1] != x2[i])
    for j in range(nout):
        tot = ev[('LEAK', j)][1] + ev[('INJ', j)][1] + ev[('RESET', j)][1]
        tot += sum(ev[('SYN', i, j)][1] for i in range(nin))
        tot += sum(c for (key, (o, c)) in ev.items() if key[0] == 'FIN' and key[2] == j)
        bad += (v[j] + tot != v2[j])
        bad += (y[j] + ev[('YDEC', j)][1] + ev[('YIN', j)][1] != y2[j])
        if ('HCOPY', j) in ev:
            bad += (h[j] + ev[('HCOPY', j)][1] != h2[j])
    return bad

def simultaneous_solutions(P, st, s, I):
    """同拍同时方程组 g_j = [base_j + Σ_k U[k][j]·g_k ≥ θ_j] 的全部解（2^n 枚举）。"""
    nout = len(st[1])
    U = P['U']
    base = _base(P, st, s, I)
    sols = []
    for g in product((0, 1), repeat=nout):
        if all((base[j] + sum((U[k][j] for k in range(nout) if g[k]), ZERO) >= P['theta'][j]) == bool(g[j]) for j in range(nout)):
            sols.append(g)
    return sols

def prop_bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0):
    """命题 M-1 的传播递推。D0 = (Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh)：拍 u 开始时副作用的逐结点上界（Dh 取 0/1，只用于 delay）。
    以设定实验读得的门控模式 g_obs 逐拍递推，只用申报参数：
      Dvt_j(t) = λ_j Dv_j + Σ_{i: s_i=1} Dw_ij + [delay] Σ_k |U_kj| Dh_k
      Dv_j(t+1) = 0（g_j = 1）或 Dvt_j(t)；Dx_i(t+1) = d_x,i Dx_i；Dy_j(t+1) = d_y,j Dy_j；Dh(t+1) = 0
      Dw_ij(t+1) = (1 − ρ·[plastic]) Dw_ij + A₊ d_x,i Dx_i·[plastic ∧ bap ∧ g_{gate(j)}] + A₋ d_y,gate(j) Dy_gate(j)·[plastic ∧ bap ∧ s_i]
    返回 (Dvt[t], Dstate[t])，Dstate[t] 为拍 t 拍前态的界，t = u..te+1（Dstate[te+1].Dw 即拍 te 夹前和 S 的差界）。"""
    Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh = [row[:] for row in D0[0]], D0[1][:], D0[2][:], D0[3][:], D0[4][:]
    nin, nout = len(Dx), len(Dv)
    mode = P.get('mode', 'g')
    U = P.get('U') or [[ZERO] * nout for _ in range(nout)]
    Dvt_all, Dst = {}, {}
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        Dst[t] = (Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh)
        s, I = seq[t]
        g = g_obs[t]
        Dvt = [P['lam'][j] * Dv[j] + sum((Dw[i][j] for i in range(nin) if s[i]), ZERO) for j in range(nout)]
        if mode == 'delay':
            Dvt = [Dvt[j] + sum((abs(U[k][j]) * Dh[k] for k in range(nout)), ZERO) for j in range(nout)]
        Dvt_all[t] = Dvt
        Dv2 = [ZERO if g[j] else Dvt[j] for j in range(nout)]
        Dx2 = [_dx(P, i) * Dx[i] for i in range(nin)]
        Dy2 = [_dy(P, j) * Dy[j] for j in range(nout)]
        coef = (1 - P['rho']) if P['plastic'] else F(1)
        Dw2 = [[None] * nout for _ in range(nin)]
        for i in range(nin):
            for j in range(nout):
                gj = P['gate_of'][j]
                a = coef * Dw[i][j]
                if P['plastic'] and P['bap'] and g[gj]:
                    a += P['Ap'] * Dx2[i]
                if P['plastic'] and P['bap'] and s[i]:
                    a += P['Am'] * Dy2[gj]
                Dw2[i][j] = a
        Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh = Dw2, Dv2, Dx2, Dy2, [0] * nout
    Dst[te + 1] = (Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh)
    return Dvt_all, Dst

def prop_bounds_no_switch(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0):
    """仅供对照：缺 plastic 开关的错误递推（权重差系数无条件取 1 − ρ）。不用于任何证书。"""
    Pn = dict(P)
    Pn['plastic'] = True
    if not P['plastic']:
        Pn['Ap'] = ZERO
        Pn['Am'] = ZERO
    return prop_bounds(Pn, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0)

def event_bound(P, e, te, Dvt_all, Dst):
    """拍 te 事件 e 的分项差上界 B_e（只以 T 设定的运行与设定实验之间），前提为 [u, te] 门控模式相同。"""
    Dw, Dv, Dx, Dy, Dh = Dst[te]
    kind = e[0]
    if kind in ('YIN', 'XIN', 'INJ'):
        return ZERO
    if kind == 'RESET':
        return Dvt_all[te][e[1]]
    if kind == 'LEAK':
        return (1 - P['lam'][e[1]]) * Dv[e[1]]
    if kind == 'SYN':
        return Dw[e[1]][e[2]]
    if kind == 'FIN':
        return abs(P['U'][e[1]][e[2]]) * Dh[e[1]] if P.get('mode') == 'delay' else ZERO
    if kind == 'HCOPY':
        return F(Dh[e[1]])
    if kind == 'XDEC':
        return (1 - _dx(P, e[1])) * Dx[e[1]]
    if kind == 'YDEC':
        return (1 - _dy(P, e[1])) * Dy[e[1]]
    if kind == 'PLUS':
        return P['Ap'] * _dx(P, e[1]) * Dx[e[1]]
    if kind == 'MINUS':
        gj = P['gate_of'][e[2]]
        return P['Am'] * _dy(P, gj) * Dy[gj]
    if kind == 'RELAX':
        return P['rho'] * Dw[e[1]][e[2]]
    if kind == 'CLIP':
        return Dst[te + 1][0][e[1]][e[2]]
    raise ValueError(kind)

def occurrence_fixed(P, e, te, Dst):
    """事件发生是否由门控、字母、开关决定（于是 [u, te] 门控模式相同 ⇒ 两运行发生相同）。"""
    if e[0] == 'CLIP':
        return False
    if e[0] == 'FIN' and P.get('mode') == 'delay' and Dst[te][4][e[1]]:
        return False
    return True

def margins_ok(P, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dvt_all):
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        for j, zz in enumerate(z_obs[t]):
            if g_obs[t][j]:
                if not (zz - rv - Dvt_all[t][j] >= P['theta'][j]):
                    return False
            else:
                if not (zz + rv + Dvt_all[t][j] < P['theta'][j]):
                    return False
    return True

def m1_certificate(P, seq, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, D0, e, ev_obs, ev_act, r=ZERO, bounds=prop_bounds):
    """命题 M-1。ev_obs、ev_act = (发生, 分项读数)，发生精确、分项读数半径 r（不发生时分项记 0）。
    裕度全部成立后：发生由门控决定的种类，发生不同 ⇒ 1；两次都发生且 |c_obs − c_act| > B_e + 2r ⇒ 1；
    CLIP 与 h 受副作用的 FIN（发生不由门控决定）：|c_obs − c_act| > B_e + 2r ⇒ 1（不发生记分项 0）。
    返回 (判词 1 或 None, 裕度是否成立)。"""
    Dvt_all, Dst = bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, D0)
    if not margins_ok(P, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dvt_all):
        return None, False
    B = event_bound(P, e, te, Dvt_all, Dst)
    (oo, co), (oa, ca) = ev_obs, ev_act
    if occurrence_fixed(P, e, te, Dst):
        if oo != oa:
            return 1, True
        if oo and oa and abs(co - ca) > B + 2 * r:
            return 1, True
        return None, True
    if abs(co - ca) > B + 2 * r:
        return 1, True
    return None, True
~~~
