~~~python
def beta_star(P, seq, u, te, z_obs, rv, g_obs, Dhat):
    """单位副作用剖面 Dhat（Dh 须为 0）下，D = κ·Dhat 时裕度条件成立的 κ 的上确界。返回 (κ*, 是否严格)：
    κ < κ*（严格）或 κ ≤ κ*（非严格）时裕度成立；κ* = None 表示对一切 κ ≥ 0 成立；κ* < 0 表示读数本身不过裕度。"""
    assert not any(Dhat[4])
    Dvt_all, _ = prop_bounds(P, seq, u, te, g_obs, Dhat)
    best, strict = None, False
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        for j, zz in enumerate(z_obs[t]):
            d = Dvt_all[t][j]
            if g_obs[t][j]:
                room = zz - rv - P['theta'][j]
                if d == 0:
                    if room < 0:
                        return F(-1), False
                    continue
                k, st_ = room / d, False
            else:
                room = P['theta'][j] - zz - rv
                if d == 0:
                    if room <= 0:
                        return F(-1), False
                    continue
                k, st_ = room / d, True
            if best is None or k < best or (k == best and st_):
                best, strict = k, st_
    return best, strict

def changed(ev1, ev2):
    (o1, c1), (o2, c2) = ev1, ev2
    return o1 != o2 or (o1 and o2 and c1 != c2)

# ───────────────────────── 命题 M-2：全局固定 pat_B 的孪生 ─────────────────────────
def m2_twin(P, st0, labels, patA, I_A, u, te, Tin, beta_v, ga_base, ga_set):
    """单输出 𝒦_g（mode 'g'）。冻结信息：标签序列 labels、两运行的发放读数 ga_base、ga_set（[0, te]）、T 的申报身份。
    Tin：T 对应的入口单元集合。构造：
      pat_B(a) := pat_A(a) 把 Tin 分量置 0（对每一标签一次定义，全程使用）；
      注入 I_B(t) 逐拍选取：t < u 时使 B 的发放等于 A 的前缀发放；u ≤ t < te 时使两运行都不发放；
      t = te 时使两运行的门控输入落在 θ 两侧，方向与 A 相同（副作用 ±beta_v 加于 v）。
    返回 dict(patB, I_B, side, base, set, used)；used[t] = 该拍实际使用的 (标签, 模式)，供逐拍核查映射一致。"""
    theta = P['theta'][0]
    patB = {a: tuple(0 if i in Tin else bit for i, bit in enumerate(p)) for a, p in patA.items()}
    flip_up = (not ga_base[te]) and ga_set[te]
    side = {('v', 0): beta_v if flip_up else -beta_v}
    I_B = []
    st = copy_state(st0)
    used = []
    # 前缀 t < u：两运行相同，逐拍选注入使门控 = A 的前缀发放
    for t in range(u):
        s = list(patB[labels[t]])
        used.append((labels[t], tuple(s)))
        b = _base(P, st, s, [ZERO])[0]
        I = theta - b if ga_base[t] else theta - b - 1
        I_B.append([I])
        st, g, vt, ev = step(P, st, s, [I])
    # u..te：两运行分开推进（同一注入）
    stb = copy_state(st)
    w, v, x, y, h = copy_state(st)
    v[0] += side[('v', 0)]
    sts = (w, v, x, y, h)
    for t in range(u, te + 1):
        s = list(patB[labels[t]])
        used.append((labels[t], tuple(s)))
        bb = _base(P, stb, s, [ZERO])[0]
        bs = _base(P, sts, s, [ZERO])[0]
        if t < te:
            I = theta - 1 - max(bb, bs)
        else:
            D = bs - bb
            if D == 0:
                return None
            I = (theta - D / 2 - bb) if D > 0 else (theta + (-D) / 2 - bb)
            # D > 0：基线 vt = θ − D/2（不发放），设定 vt = θ + D/2（发放）；D < 0 反向
        I_B.append([I])
        stb, _, _, _ = step(P, stb, s, [I])
        sts, _, _, _ = step(P, sts, s, [I])
    seqB = [(list(patB[labels[t]]), I_B[t]) for t in range(te + 1)]
    return {'patB': patB, 'I_B': I_B, 'seqB': seqB, 'side': side, 'used': used}
~~~

A.10　run\_main.py　运行入口：版本绑定与实现对照、实例 P、穷尽与随机核验、K3 第二款、网格与随机族、去条件见证、充分非必要、线性族、紧性构造（S0—S8、S3b）

~~~python
"""《场具化资格必然式》附录程序：主核验（S0—S8，S3b）。只用标准库，精确有理运算；随机种子固定，输出确定。
运行（在本目录）：python3 run_main.py
程序给出的是有限核验与显式见证；全称结论以正文的证明为准。
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, itertools
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
import t14_core as C
from t14_core import fr
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S0 版本绑定
APPX_B = {  # 第十一篇 v1.0 附录 B.1 所列 SHA-256（附录 A 逐字取出）
    'snn_core.py': '0c750fb5f57b52c40ff30feb7734c120096ffd7267e6ddc723d163cc0d1b4ceb',
    'gate_class.py': '3b9bf68fa128bc27f2600ec35eb697b466ba3248013b9e1becae3bfffd7754e4',
    'r_layer_witness.py': '50785e9f6790f3047595b9bc40b23186e781a19976ac871cf8caf41aff4cdf6f',
    'gate_criteria.py': '4db576950ed993a730f81325099195fc10ee119a0e65ed9d9c878142de8698c5',
    'lp_exact.py': '1c03a3fcc9d7a9b3065bdfcab466c6395f4e4b23b79be64a370f6712749abeda',
    'decision.py': '5aefc70dfc911e29cef1f7ca390d6ef36638266f8c8d3be9bc8c85fe8a98da81',
}

def sha(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

def s0():
    print('S0　版本绑定与独立实现对照')
    for f, h in APPX_B.items():
        got = sha(os.path.join(HERE, f))
        assert got == h, f
        print(f'  {f}  SHA-256 {got[:16]}…  与第十一篇附录 B.1 相符')
    import gate_class as GC
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    NONE = ('none', (0, 0))
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    P = FORM + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0()
    rng = random.Random(20261103)
    hists = [P]
    for _ in range(300):
        hists.append([(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]), rng.choice([F(0), F(1)])))
                      for _ in range(60)])
    beats = bad = clip = 0
    for hst in hists:
        st = S.init(); o = M.init(); og = G.init()
        for (inp, teach) in hst:
            st, ev = S.step(st, inp, teach)
            o, r = M.step(o, (inp, teach))
            og, rg = G.step(og, (inp, teach))
            beats += 1
            if not (st['w'] == o[0] == og[0] and st['v'] == o[1] == og[1] and st['x'] == o[2] == og[2] and st['y'] == o[3] == og[3]):
                bad += 1
            if tuple(ev['s_out']) != tuple(r['g']):
                bad += 1
            for (i, j), (pos, neg, rel, d) in ev['comp'].items():
                if pos != r['ev'].get(('PLUS', i, j), F(0)) or neg != r['ev'].get(('MINUS', i, j), F(0)):
                    bad += 1
                if ('CLIP', i, j) in r['ev']:
                    clip += 1
    print(f'  冻结 snn_core.step（SHA-256 {h[:16]}…）、第十一篇附录 A.2 Gate.step 与本篇 Mem.step：{len(hists)} 条史、{beats} 拍，'
          f'全状态与 PLUS/MINUS 分项不一致 {bad}；其中夹限生效 {clip} 处')
    # 随机成员：Mem 与 Gate
    bad2 = n2 = 0
    for _ in range(200):
        n_in, n_out = rng.choice([2, 3, 4]), rng.choice([1, 2])
        pats = {'none': (0,) * n_in}
        for kk in range(3):
            pats[f'p{kk}'] = tuple(rng.choice([0, 1]) for _ in range(n_in))
        q = lambda: F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8)
        b = [[F(rng.randint(0, 4), 8) for _ in range(n_out)] for _ in range(n_in)]
        lo = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        hi = [[F(2)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        args = dict(n_in=n_in, n_out=n_out, pat=pats, lam=[q() for _ in range(n_out)], theta=[F(1)] * n_out,
                    vreset=[F(0)] * n_out, dx=[q() for _ in range(n_in)], dy=[q() for _ in range(n_out)],
                    Ap=F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16), Am=F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16), rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10)]), b=b, lo=lo, hi=hi)
        M2 = C.Mem(**args); G2 = GC.Gate(**args)
        o, og = M2.init(), G2.init()
        for _ in range(40):
            sig = (rng.choice(list(pats)), tuple(rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)]) for _ in range(n_out)))
            o, r = M2.step(o, sig); og, rg = G2.step(og, sig)
            n2 += 1
            if o != og or tuple(r['g']) != tuple(rg['g']):
                bad2 += 1
    print(f'  随机 𝒦_g 成员 200 个（含 ρ = 1/10）各 40 拍，共 {n2} 拍：Mem 与 Gate 全状态不一致 {bad2}')
    print()
    return GC, S

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S1 实例 P
NONE2 = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))
FORM_P = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * 6
WIN0 = [('A', (F(0), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2, ('B', (F(0), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * 3
HIST_P = FORM_P + [NONE2] * 8 + WIN0

def show_C(items):
    for name, v, d in items:
        print(f'    [{"成立" if v else "不成立"}] {name}' + (f'　{d}' if d else ''))

def s1():
    print('S1　实例 P（s1 冻结申报史；第十一篇 7.5（一））：C 的逐项核查、制度核查与见证')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    j, A, n, mF = 0, [0, 1], 6, 2
    m = C.min_margin(M, j, A)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)
    print(f'  申报：j = 输出 0，A = {{入口 0, 入口 1}}，T = {{w00, w10}}，n = {n}，m_F = {mF}，K3 的最小静置裕度 m = {m}')
    show_C(items)
    print(f'  C（系数与初始资源部分）：{"全部成立" if ok else "不成立"}；δ_F = {fr(der["delta_F"])}，ω = ' +
          ', '.join(f'w{i}0 ≥ {fr(v)}' for i, v in der['omega'].items()))
    W = (26, 43)
    rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, HIST_P, W, n, mF, m)
    print(f'  制度：{"属 𝒟" if rg["ok"] else "不属 𝒟：" + "；".join(rg["why"])}；配对拍 {rg["pairs"]}；W = 拍 26—43 内净探针拍 {rg["probes"]}')
    V = C.verify(M, j, A, HIST_P, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'])
    print(f'  不变量 I1—I4 违例：{len(V["inv"])}')
    print('  形成期末 w：' + ', '.join(f'w{i}0 = {fr(v)}' for i, v in V['w_after_form'].items()) +
          f'（下界 ω = {fr(der["omega"][0])}；上界 b + n·Δ̄_F = {fr(M.b[0][0] + n * der["Dbar_F"])}）')
    rd = V['r_detail']
    print(f'  r：首个作用于 T 的有效写入在拍 {rd["t"]}（{rd["n_eff"]} 个）；F3 = {rd["mode"]}，同拍撤除 {rd["withdrawal"][0]}、I = {tuple(fr(c) for c in rd["withdrawal"][1])}：'
          f'无开启 = {rd["noopen"]}，改变的事件 {len(rd["changed"])} 个 ⇒ M1₀，r = {V["r"]}（P4R = {rd["P4R"]}，只作可选充分前件，未用）')
    print('  q：窗末 z = ' + ', '.join(f'w{k[0]}0 {fr(v)}' for k, v in V['z_end'].items()) +
          f'；形成记录拍 {V["form"][(0, 0)][:1]}、{V["form"][(1, 0)][:1]}；q = {V["q"]}')
    pb = V['probe']
    print(f'  探针拍 {pb["t"]}：实际 vt = {fr(pb["vt"])}，发放 {pb["g"]}；𝒟₁ 设定（u = {pb["t"]}，T 各位设为 b = 1/4）后 vt = {fr(pb["vt_cf"])}，发放 {pb["g_cf"]}')
    print('    PLUS 分项：' + ', '.join(f'{k[0]}{k[1]}{k[2]} = {fr(c)}' for k, c in pb['plus_c'].items()) +
          f'；有效且随设定改变者 {len(pb["fd_plus"])} 个；门控读取集含 T 位（Path_act）= {pb["path"]}；SYN 随设定改变 {len(pb["syn_fd"])} 个；饱和位 {pb["sat"]}')
    print(f'  ⇒ Part 见证 = {V["part"]}，HDyn 见证 = {V["hdyn"]}，R(T) = q ∧ r ∧ Part ∧ HDyn = {V["R"]}')
    xs = der['x_sup']
    bound = M.b[0][0] + n * der['Dbar_F'] + M.Ap * xs
    print(f'  推论 14-1′ 不饱和条件：b + n·Δ̄_F + A₊·x_sup = {fr(bound)} ≤ hi = {fr(M.hi[0][0])}：{bound <= M.hi[0][0]}')
    print('  对照第十一篇 7.5（一）的读数：窗末 z = 0.607871、PLUS00@26 = 0.125070、Fld = 1（算法层）。')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S2 穷尽核验（冻结成员）
def s2():
    print('S2　冻结成员上的穷尽制度核验：形成（P 的 6 次配对）＋间奏 2 拍＋静置 2 拍＋探针＋尾 3 拍；')
    print('    间奏与尾的每拍取 {none, A, B, AB} × 教学电流 I₀ ∈ {0, 1}（I₁ = 0），共 8^5 = 32768 条；W = 间奏首拍至末拍')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    j, A, n, mF, m = 0, [0, 1], 6, 2, 2
    letters = [(nm, (F(i0), F(0))) for nm in ['none', 'A', 'B', 'AB'] for i0 in (0, 1)]
    form = FORM_P
    cnt = dict(runs=0, R=0, inv=0, sat=0, noC=0, multi=0)
    vmin = None
    for combo in itertools.product(letters, repeat=5):
        inter, tail = list(combo[:2]), list(combo[2:])
        hist = form + inter + [NONE2, NONE2, ('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + tail
        W = (len(form), len(hist) - 1)
        tp = len(form) + 4
        rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
        if not rg['ok'] or tp not in rg['probes']:
            cnt['noC'] += 1; continue
        V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'], probe=tp)
        cnt['runs'] += 1
        cnt['R'] += V['R'] == 1
        cnt['inv'] += bool(V['inv'])
        cnt['sat'] += bool(V['probe']['sat'])
        cnt['multi'] += len(rg['probes']) > 1
        mz = min(V['z_end'].values())
        vmin = mz if vmin is None else min(vmin, mz)
    print(f'  属 𝒟 的轨迹 {cnt["runs"]} 条（制度核查未通过 {cnt["noC"]}）；R(T) 见证成立 {cnt["R"]}；不变量违例的轨迹 {cnt["inv"]}；'
          f'探针拍 T 位饱和 {cnt["sat"]}；W 内多于一个净探针拍 {cnt["multi"]}；窗末 min z = {fr(vmin)}')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S3 随机成员（满足 C）与随机轨迹
def rand_member(rng, need=True, eps=F(1, 100)):
    """生成 𝒦_LS 成员；need=True 时调整参数使 K1—K3、F0 成立（F2 由选 n 满足）。"""
    while True:
        n_in = rng.choice([2, 3, 4]); n_out = rng.choice([1, 2])
        a_sz = rng.randint(1, n_in - 1) if n_in > 1 else 1
        A = sorted(rng.sample(range(n_in), a_sz))
        others = [i for i in range(n_in) if i not in A]
        pats = {'none': (0,) * n_in, 'pA': tuple(1 if i in A else 0 for i in range(n_in))}
        for kk in range(rng.randint(1, 3)):
            withA = rng.choice([0, 1])
            pats[f'q{kk}'] = tuple(withA if i in A else rng.choice([0, 1]) for i in range(n_in))
        th = F(rng.choice([1, 1, 2, 3]), rng.choice([1, 2]))
        lam = F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8)
        dy = F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8); dx = [F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8) for _ in range(n_in)]
        Am = F(rng.randint(0, 6), 32)
        Ap = max(Am / (1 - dy), eps) + F(rng.randint(1, 6), 64)
        m = rng.randint(0, 6)
        room = th - lam ** (m + 1) * th
        if room <= 0:
            continue
        b = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        for i in A:
            b[i][0] = room / len(A) * F(rng.randint(1, 8), 8)
        for i in others:
            b[i][0] = F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8) * th
        for jj in range(1, n_out):
            for i in range(n_in):
                b[i][jj] = F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8)
        hi = [[b[i][jj] + F(rng.randint(1, 16), 4) * th for jj in range(n_out)] for i in range(n_in)]
        lo = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        M = C.Mem(n_in, n_out, pats, [lam] * n_out, [th] * n_out, [0] * n_out, dx, [dy] * n_out, Ap, Am, 0, b, lo, hi, eps)
        mF = rng.randint(0, 4)
        ok0, items, der = C.check_C(M, 0, A, 1, mF, m)
        dF = der['delta_F']
        if dF is None or dF <= 0:
            continue
        n = 1
        while True:
            ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, 0, A, n, mF, m)
            if ok or n > 400:
                break
            n += 1
        if not ok:
            continue
        n += rng.randint(0, 3)
        return M, A, n, mF, m

def rand_traj(rng, M, j, A, n, mF, m, inter_len, tail_len, gap_extra=3):
    th = M.theta[j]; no = M.n_out
    zero = tuple(F(0) for _ in range(no))
    def quiet():
        return ('none', tuple(F(0) if jj == j else F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2) for jj in range(no)))
    withA = [nm for nm, p in M.pat.items() if all(p[i] for i in A)]
    hist = []
    for k in range(n):
        if k:
            hist += [quiet() for _ in range(mF + rng.randint(0, gap_extra))]
        else:
            hist += [quiet() for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 2))]
        hist.append((rng.choice(withA), tuple(th + F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4) if jj == j else F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4) for jj in range(no))))
    pn = len(hist) - 1
    def anylet():
        r = rng.random()
        I = tuple(rng.choice([F(0), F(0), th / 2, th, 2 * th]) for _ in range(no))
        return (rng.choice(list(M.pat)), I)
    hist += [anylet() for _ in range(inter_len)]
    hist += [quiet() for _ in range(m + rng.randint(0, 2))]
    tp = len(hist)
    hist.append(('pA', tuple(F(0) if jj == j else F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2) for jj in range(no))))
    hist += [anylet() for _ in range(tail_len)]
    ts = rng.randint(pn + 1, tp)
    return hist, (ts, len(hist) - 1), tp

def s3():
    print('S3　随机 𝒦_LS 成员（满足 C）× 随机轨迹（属 𝒟）：动力不变量与职责见证')
    rng = random.Random(20261104)
    stats = dict(mem=0, traj=0, R=0, inv=0, sat=0, nsf=0, nin={}, asz={})
    for _ in range(300):
        M, A, n, mF, m = rand_member(rng)
        stats['mem'] += 1
        stats['nin'][M.n_in] = stats['nin'].get(M.n_in, 0) + 1
        stats['asz'][len(A)] = stats['asz'].get(len(A), 0) + 1
        for _ in range(5):
            hist, W, tp = rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 40), rng.randint(0, 40))
            rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
            assert rg['ok'] and tp in rg['probes'], rg['why']
            V = C.verify(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'], probe=tp)
            stats['traj'] += 1
            stats['R'] += V['R'] == 1
            stats['inv'] += bool(V['inv'])
            stats['sat'] += bool(V['probe']['sat'])
            stats['nsf'] += not V['probe']['ns_star']
            if V['R'] != 1 or V['inv']:
                print('  反例：', V['inv'][:3], V['q'], V['r'], V['part'], V['hdyn'])
    print(f'  成员 {stats["mem"]} 个（入口数分布 {dict(sorted(stats["nin"].items()))}，|A| 分布 {dict(sorted(stats["asz"].items()))}），'
          f'轨迹 {stats["traj"]} 条（长度至 {""}约 120 拍，含任意字母与 I ∈ {{0, θ/2, θ, 2θ}} 的间奏与尾）')
    print(f'  R(T) 见证成立 {stats["R"]} / {stats["traj"]}；不变量违例 {stats["inv"]}；探针拍有 T 位夹限 {stats["sat"]}，'
          f'其中 T 各位都已在 hi − ε 之上（NS* 不成立，须携 E11）{stats["nsf"]}')
    print()

def s3b():
    print('S3b　K3 第二款的必要性：只含第一款（λ^(m+1)θ + Σ_A b ≤ θ）的弱化条件在 λ = 0 时失去严格性')
    # （一）以只含第一款的 K3 重放 S3 的同一生成（种子 20261104），归类未得见证的轨迹
    orig = C.check_C
    def weak_k3(M, j, A, n, mF, m, **kw):
        ok, items, der = orig(M, j, A, n, mF, m, **kw)
        items = [(nm, (C.k3_first_clause(M, j, A, m) if nm.startswith('K3') else v), d) for nm, v, d in items]
        return all(v for _, v, _ in items), items, der
    C.check_C = weak_k3
    try:
        rng = random.Random(20261104)
        tot = miss = cls = 0
        for _ in range(300):
            M, A, n, mF, m = rand_member(rng)
            for _ in range(5):
                hist, W, tp = rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 40), rng.randint(0, 40))
                rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
                V = C.verify(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'], probe=tp)
                tot += 1
                if V['R'] != 1:
                    miss += 1
                    sb = sum(M.b[i][0] for i in A)
                    cls += (M.lam[0] == 0 and sb == M.theta[0] and V['q'] == 1 and V['r'] == 1 and V['part'] == 1
                            and V['probe']['g_cf'] == 1)
    finally:
        C.check_C = orig
    print(f'  以只含第一款的 K3 重放 S3 的生成：轨迹 {tot} 条，未得 R 见证 {miss} 条；其中「λ = 0 且 Σ_A b = θ，q、r、Part 成立，探针拍上 T 设为 b 后门控仍开启」{cls} 条')
    # （二）最小显式例
    th = F(2)
    M = C.Mem(2, 1, {'none': (0, 0), 'pA': (0, 1), 'q0': (1, 0)}, [F(0)], [th], [0], [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)], F(3, 8), F(1, 16), 0,
              [[F(1, 2)], [th]], [[F(0)], [F(0)]], [[F(4)], [F(5)]])
    A, m, n, mF = [1], 0, 1, 1
    first = C.k3_first_clause(M, 0, A, m)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, 0, A, n, mF, m)
    Q = ('none', (F(0),))
    hist = [('pA', (th,)), Q, Q, ('pA', (F(0),)), Q]
    W = (3, 4)
    rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'], probe=3)
    pb = V['probe']
    print(f'  最小显式例：λ = 0，θ = 2，A = {{入口 1}}，b₁ = 2 = θ；只含第一款的 K3(m=0)：{first}；完整 K3：{[v for nm, v, d in items if nm.startswith("K3")][0]}')
    print(f'    探针拍 3：实际 vt = {fr(pb["vt"])}；T 设为 b 后 vt = {fr(pb["vt_cf"])} ≥ θ，门控仍开启 ⇒ 无 HDyn 见证（{V["hdyn"]}）')
    print('  原因：证明中 vt_cf = λ·v_pre + Σ_A b < λ^(m+1)θ + Σ_A b 用到 v_pre < λ^m θ 的严格性，λ = 0 时乘积为 0，严格性丢失。')
    print('  故 K3 取两款「λ^(m+1)θ + Σ_A b ≤ θ 且 Σ_A b < θ」（λ > 0 时后者由前者推出）；S3 按完整 K3 生成。')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S4 第十一篇网格 108 组与随机 200 条
def s4():
    print('S4　第十一篇网格 108 组（W0 × A₊ × 教学 × n；P 型史）上的 C 核查与见证；随机输入族 200 条的制度分类')
    j, A, mF = 0, [0, 1], 2
    rows = []
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)],
                                              [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]):
        M = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=W0, Ap=AP)
        form = [('A', (teach, F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * n
        hist = form + [NONE2] * 8 + WIN0
        W = (len(form) + 8, len(hist) - 1)
        m = C.min_margin(M, j, A)
        okC = m is not None and C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)[0]
        rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m if m is not None else 0)
        V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m if m is not None else 0, pairs=rg['pairs'] if rg['pairs'] else None)
        rows.append((W0, AP, teach, n, okC and rg['ok'], V['R'], V['q'], V['hdyn']))
    inC = [r for r in rows if r[4]]
    print(f'  C ∧ 制度成立的组：{len(inC)}；其中 R(T) 见证成立 {sum(1 for r in inC if r[5] == 1)}')
    for r in inC:
        print(f'    W0 = {fr(r[0])}，A₊ = {fr(r[1])}，教学 = {fr(r[2])}，n = {r[3]}：R 见证 = {r[5]}')
    outC = [r for r in rows if not r[4]]
    print(f'  C 或制度不成立的组：{len(outC)}；其中本程序仍得 R 见证（充分但非必要）{sum(1 for r in outC if r[5] == 1)}，未得见证 {sum(1 for r in outC if r[5] != 1)}（未得见证不读作 0）')
    # 第十一篇 lp03_exact 的随机输入族（同一生成与种子）
    rng = random.Random(20261031)
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    noA = clean = Aonly = 0
    form = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * 6
    for kk in range(200):
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'none', 'none']), (F(0), F(0))) for _ in range(18)]
        hist = form + [NONE2] * 8 + win
        W = (26, 43)
        rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, hist, W, 6, mF, 2)
        hasA = any(s[0] == 'A' for s in win)
        noA += not hasA
        clean += rg['ok']
        Aonly += hasA and not rg['ok']
    print(f'  随机输入族 200 条（第十一篇 lp03_exact 第二部分，种子 20261031）：W 内无 A 字母 {noA} 条；有净探针拍（属 𝒟）{clean} 条；有 A 而无净探针拍 {Aonly} 条')
    print('    对照第十一篇附录 B.2 第二项：Part = 0、HDyn = 0 共 4 条，其余 196 条 Part = 1、HDyn = 1。')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S5 去条件见证（C 某项失效而 R(T) 失效）
def run_one(M, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, title, j=0):
    rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
    V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'] if len(rg['pairs']) == n else None)
    return rg, V

def erosion_hist(M, A, n, mF, single=None, K1=12, K2=12, cycles=200, teach_at_A=True):
    """命题 14-3 的构造：形成后一个净探针拍，再重复〔静置 K2 拍 → 教学电流单独发放 K1 拍 → A（或单入口字母）〕。"""
    th = M.theta[0]
    Q = ('none', (F(0),) * M.n_out)
    hist = []
    for k in range(n):
        hist += [Q] * (mF if k else 0) + [('A', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M.n_out - 1))]
    hist += [Q] * 3 + [('A', (F(0),) * M.n_out)]
    ts = len(hist) - 1
    lt = single if single else 'A'
    for _ in range(cycles):
        hist += [Q] * K2 + [('none', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M.n_out - 1))] * K1
        hist += [(lt, ((th if teach_at_A else F(0)),) + (F(0),) * (M.n_out - 1))]
    return hist, ts

def s5():
    print('S5　去条件见证：只破坏 C 的一项，其余各项成立；示所申报 T 上 R(T) 失效（HDyn 的判词另由 run_xcheck 以第十一篇判定程序核对）')
    j, A, mF = 0, [0, 1], 2
    # D-K3：静置裕度 m = 1 时 K3 不成立；预载膜电位
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    m = 1
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, 6, mF, m)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    hist = FORM_P + [NONE2] * 3 + [('none', (F(15, 16), F(0))), NONE2, ('A', (F(0), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2]
    W = (len(hist) - 3, len(hist) - 1)
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist, W, 6, mF, m, 'D-K3')
    pb = V['probe'] if 'probe' in V else None
    tp = len(hist) - 3
    o = V['pre'][tp]
    vt_b = M.lam[0] * o[1][0] + 2 * M.b[0][0]
    print(f'  D-K3（m = 1）：不成立项 {bad}；制度 {rg["ok"]}；探针拍 {tp} 前 v = {fr(o[1][0])}，T 设为 b 时 vt = {fr(vt_b)} ≥ θ：'
          f'门控在 𝒟₁ 的一切设定下开启（vt 对 σ 单调不减）；本程序探针见证 HDyn = {V["hdyn"]}，q = {V["q"]}，r = {V["r"]}，Part = {V["part"]}')
    # D-K3′：W0 = 1/2，Σ_A b = θ
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=F(1, 2))
    mm = C.min_margin(M, j, A)
    print(f'  D-K3′（W0 = 1/2，Σ_A b = θ）：K3 对一切 m 不成立（min_margin = {mm}）；P 型史上探针拍 T 设为 b 时 vt ≥ 1；第十一篇网格同组 HDyn 为第（b）档 0 [B_FD]／⊥_Over')
    # D-K2：A₋ = 1/12
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(Am=F(1, 12))
    m = C.min_margin(M, j, A)
    n = 1
    while not all(v for nm, v, d in C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)[1] if not nm.startswith('K2')):
        n += 1
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    hist, ts = erosion_hist(M, A, n, mF)
    pre, recs = M.run(hist)
    zs = [min(o[0][i][0] - M.b[i][0] for i in A) for o in pre]
    te = next(t for t in range(ts + 1, len(hist)) if zs[t + 1] <= M.eps)
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist[:te + 1], (ts, te), n, mF, m, 'D-K2')
    print(f'  D-K2（A₋ = 1/12，A₋/(1−d_y) = 1/6 > A₊ = 1/8；n = {n}）：不成立项 {bad}；制度 {rg["ok"]}（W = 拍 {ts}—{te}）；'
          f'形成后 Σ_A w = {fr(sum(pre[ts][0][i][0] for i in A))}；窗末 z = {", ".join(fr(v) for v in V["z_end"].values())} ⇒ q = {V["q"]}（R(T) 不成立：T 位窗末未留存）')
    # D-F2：n = 3
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    m = 2
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, 3, mF, m)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    form3 = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * 3
    hist = form3 + [NONE2] * 8 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + [NONE2] * 5
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist, (len(form3) + 8, len(hist) - 1), 3, mF, m, 'D-F2')
    pb = V.get('probe')
    print(f'  D-F2（n = 3；W = 一个净探针拍＋静置 5 拍）：不成立项 {bad}；制度 {rg["ok"]}；形成期末 Σ_A w = {fr(sum(V["pre"][len(form3)][0][i][0] for i in A))} < θ；'
          f'W 内 T 位事件：' + summarize_T_events(V, A, (len(form3) + 8, len(hist) - 1), M.eps) + f'；HDyn 见证 = {V["hdyn"]}')
    # D-S3：ρ = 1/10，长静置尾
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(rho=F(1, 10))
    hist = FORM_P + [NONE2] * 8 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + [NONE2] * 60
    W = (26, len(hist) - 1)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, 6, mF, 2)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist, W, 6, mF, 2, 'D-S3')
    print(f'  D-S3（ρ = 1/10，探针后静置 60 拍）：不成立项 {bad}；制度 {rg["ok"]}；窗末 z = {", ".join(fr(v) for v in V["z_end"].values())} ⇒ q = {V["q"]}'
          f'（另：RELAX 读本位迹应，W 内弛豫分项即可使 HDyn 判 1——这是 C 排除的退浅路线，见正文 6.8）')
    # D-S4：字母表含单入口字母 A0 = (1,0,0,0)
    pat = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'A0': (1, 0, 0, 0), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}
    full = lambda q: [[q] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
    M = C.Mem(4, 2, pat, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [1, 1], [0, 0], [F(1, 2)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), F(1, 16), 0, full(F(1, 4)), full(F(0)), full(F(2)))
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, 6, mF, 2)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    hist, ts = erosion_hist(M, A, 6, mF, single='A0', teach_at_A=False, cycles=60)
    pre, recs = M.run(hist)
    zs = [pre[t][0][0][0] - M.b[0][0] for t in range(len(pre))]
    te = next((t for t in range(ts + 1, len(hist)) if zs[t + 1] <= M.eps), len(hist) - 1)
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist[:te + 1], (ts, te), 6, mF, 2, 'D-S4')
    print(f'  D-S4（字母表含单入口字母 A0）：不成立项 {bad}；窗末 z = {", ".join(fr(v) for v in V["z_end"].values())} ⇒ q = {V["q"]}（w00 被单入口字母上的抑制分项侵蚀）')
    # D-W：W 内无净探针拍（全静置）
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    hist = FORM_P + [NONE2] * 8 + [NONE2] * 6
    rg, V = run_one(M, A, hist, (26, 31), 6, mF, 2, 'D-W')
    print(f'  D-W（W 全静置）：制度 {rg["ok"]}（{"；".join(rg["why"])}）；W 内作用于 T 的事件 {count_T_events(V, A, (26, 31))} 个 ⇒ HDyn 无承担者（a = 0），Part 无 B 内条件过程')
    print()

def count_T_events(V, A, W):
    return sum(1 for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1) for key in V['recs'][t]['ev'] if key[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP') and key[1] in A and key[2] == 0)

def summarize_T_events(V, A, W, eps):
    tot = eff = 0
    for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1):
        for key, c in V['recs'][t]['ev'].items():
            if key[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP') and key[1] in A and key[2] == 0:
                tot += 1; eff += abs(c) > eps
    return f'{tot} 个，其中有效（|c| > ε）{eff} 个'

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S6 充分但非必要（R 成立而 C 某项不成立）
def s6():
    print('S6　充分但非必要的见证（同族、同制度类、同层级与分辨率申报）：C 的某项不成立而 R(T) 见证成立')
    j, A, mF = 0, [0, 1], 2
    cases = [
        ('教学 1/2 < θ（F1 配对拍要求 I ≥ θ 不成立）', C.frozen_snn_member(), F(1, 2), 6),
        ('n = 4（F2a 不成立：δ_F 是保守下界，实际每次配对增量 ≥ 1/16）', C.frozen_snn_member(), F(1), 4),
    ]
    for title, M, teach, n in cases:
        form = [('A', (teach, F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * n
        hist = form + [NONE2] * 8 + WIN0
        W = (len(form) + 8, len(hist) - 1)
        m = C.min_margin(M, j, A)
        ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)
        rg = C.check_regime(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
        bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v] + rg['why']
        pairs = [3 * k for k in range(n)]
        V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=pairs)
        print(f'  {title}：不成立项 {bad}；R(T) 见证 = {V["R"]}（q = {V["q"]}，r = {V["r"]}，Part = {V["part"]}，HDyn = {V["hdyn"]}）')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    form3 = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * 3
    hist = form3 + [NONE2] * 8 + WIN0
    W = (len(form3) + 8, len(hist) - 1)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, 3, mF, 2)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, 3, mF, 2, pairs=[0, 3, 6])
    fired = [t for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1) if V['recs'][t]['g'][0] == 1]
    pb = V['probe']
    print(f'  n = 3 在 P 型窗（A，静，静，B，静，静）×3 上：不成立项 {bad}；首个探针不发放；拍 {fired} 发放——前一 B 拍未发放留下的膜电位与迹应合力越阈，'
          f'设 T 为 b 后 vt = {fr(pb["vt_cf"])} < θ；R(T) 见证 = {V["R"]}（与 S5 D-F2 同一成员：单探针窗上 R 失效）')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(Am=F(1, 12))
    m = C.min_margin(M, j, A); n = 9
    form = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE2, NONE2] * n
    hist = form + [NONE2] * 8 + WIN0
    W = (len(form) + 8, len(hist) - 1)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, j, A, n, mF, m)
    bad = [nm for nm, v, d in items if not v]
    V = C.verify(M, j, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=[3 * k for k in range(n)])
    print(f'  A₋ = 1/12（K2 不成立，与 S5 D-K2 同一成员）在 P 型窗上：不成立项 {bad}；R(T) 见证 = {V["R"]}——同一成员在别的合法轨迹上 R 失效（S5），故 K2 对制度类全称是必需的，对个别轨迹不是')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S7 线性族（族甲、族戊）
def s7():
    print('S7　族一（族甲参数）与族二（族戊参数）：线性 F 族 M(a, b, k, γ, ε) 上的对照引理 14-L（直接推广，登记为引理级）')
    a, b, k, g, eps = F(2), F(1, 10), F(1, 10), F(1, 10), F(1, 1000)
    for phys, label in (('甲', '族甲（恒压头流网，非神经）'), ('戊', '族戊（发放率 Hebb，神经）')):
        L = Lin(a, b, k, g, eps, phys=phys)
        tot = Rn = leg = same = allrest = allrest_R = 0
        predicted = bad = 0
        for Ln in range(1, 11):
            zall = a
            for _ in range(Ln):
                zall = zall * (1 + k - g) + b
            for seq in itertools.product(['drive', 'rest'], repeat=Ln):
                hist = ['seed'] + list(seq)
                for ts in range(1, Ln + 1):
                    for te in (Ln,):
                        W = (ts, te)
                        res = L.run(hist, W)
                        tot += 1
                        cond = (a > eps and a * (1 - g) ** te > eps and g * a * (1 - g) ** (ts - 1) > eps
                                and (zall <= L.zmax if phys == '甲' else zall <= L.g1 * L.R0 - 0 and 1 + zall <= L.ymax))
                        predicted += cond
                        if cond and not (res['R'] and res['legal']):
                            bad += 1
                        Rn += res['R']; leg += res['legal']; same += res['same']
                        if all(s == 'rest' for s in seq[ts - 1:te]):
                            allrest += 1; allrest_R += res['R']
        print(f'  {label}：史「seed + {{drive, rest}}^L」，L = 1—10，窗取 [ts, L]（ts = 1—L），共 {tot} 例；C_lin 成立 {predicted} 例，其中 R ∧ 合法 不成立 {bad} 例；'
              f'合法 {leg}；F 层与机制层轨迹逐拍相同 {same}；W 全为 rest 的 {allrest} 例中 R = 1 有 {allrest_R} 例（由弛豫分项承担）')
    # 去条件
    L = Lin(a, b, k, g, eps, phys='戊', R0=10)
    r = L.run(['seed', 'drive'], (1, 1))
    print(f'  族戊受体池初值 10：形式 R = {r["R"]}，物理合法 = {r["legal"]}（受体池 {fr(r["pool"])} < 0；构造证书不发，属物理合法项失效，不是 R 在合法实例上失败）')
    L = Lin(F(1, 2000), b, k, g, eps)
    r = L.run(['seed', 'drive', 'rest'], (1, 2))
    print(f'  a = 1/2000 ≤ ε：r = {r["r"]}，R = {r["R"]}')
    L = Lin(a, b, k, g, eps)
    r = L.run(['seed'] + ['rest'] * 80 + ['drive'], (81, 81))
    zpre = a * (1 - g) ** 80
    print(f'  形成后静置 80 拍再驱动一拍（W = 驱动拍）：F 层 R = {r["R"]}，分辨率相容 = {r["compat"]}，F 层与机制层相同 = {r["same"]}；'
          f'机制层驱动拍前 z₁ = 2·(9/10)^80 = {fr(zpre)} ≤ ε，z₁ 不是可用位，写入与弛豫在机制层都无 HistCond（F 层冻结的亚分辨率留存使其误读为可用；'
          f'第六篇命题二、三：m > 50 不相容，F 读数不读回）；C_lin 的「γ·a(1−γ)^(ts−1) > ε」不成立')
    L = Lin(a, b, k, F(0), eps)
    r1 = L.run(['seed', 'rest', 'rest'], (1, 2)); r2 = L.run(['seed', 'rest', 'drive'], (1, 2))
    print(f'  γ = 0：W 全 rest 时 HDyn = {r1["hdyn"]}；W 含 drive（k ≠ 0）时 HDyn = {r2["hdyn"]}——即命题一的分项条件在制度类上的逐拍推广')
    print()

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S8 紧性构造（命题 14-2、14-3）于随机成员
def s8():
    print('S8　紧性构造于随机成员：K3 不成立（其余成立）时的预载构造；K2 不成立（其余成立）时的侵蚀构造')
    rng = random.Random(20261105)
    k3n = k3ok = k3clip = 0
    k2n = k2ok = 0
    tries = 0
    while (k3n < 60 or k2n < 30) and tries < 20000:
        tries += 1
        M, A, n, mF, m = rand_member(rng)
        j = 0; th = M.theta[0]; lam = M.lam[0]
        if k3n < 60 and m >= 1 and lam > 0:
            mm = m - 1      # 以 m − 1 为申报裕度，则 K3 不成立
            sb = sum(M.b[i][0] for i in A)
            vstar = (th - sb) / lam ** (mm + 1)
            if vstar < th:
                c = (vstar + th) / 2
                Q = ('none', (F(0),) * M.n_out)
                hist = []
                for kk in range(n):
                    hist += [Q] * (mF if kk else 0) + [('pA', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M.n_out - 1))]
                hist += [Q] * 2 + [('none', (c,) + (F(0),) * (M.n_out - 1))] + [Q] * mm + [('pA', (F(0),) * M.n_out)] + [Q] * 2
                tp = len(hist) - 3
                pre, recs = M.run(hist)
                o = pre[tp]
                vt_b = lam * o[1][0] + sb
                k3n += 1
                noclip = not any(('CLIP', i, 0) in recs[tp]['ev'] for i in A)
                k3clip += not noclip
                k3ok += vt_b >= th and recs[tp]['g'][0] == 1 and noclip
        if k2n < 30 and M.dy[0] > 0 and min(M.dx[i] for i in A) < 1:
            # 把 A₋ 提到使 K2 恰不成立，再按 F2 重选 n
            ysup = 1 / (1 - M.dy[0])
            Am2 = M.Ap / ysup * F(5, 4)
            M2 = C.Mem(M.n_in, M.n_out, M.pat, M.lam, M.theta, M.vreset, M.dx, M.dy, M.Ap, Am2, 0, M.b, M.lo, M.hi, M.eps)
            dF = C.check_C(M2, 0, A, 1, mF, m)[2]['delta_F']
            if dF is None or dF <= 0:
                continue
            n2 = 1
            while not all(v for nm, v, d in C.check_C(M2, 0, A, n2, mF, m)[1] if not nm.startswith('K2')) and n2 < 2000:
                n2 += 1
            if n2 >= 2000:
                continue
            th = M2.theta[0]
            Q = ('none', (F(0),) * M2.n_out)
            hist = []
            for kk in range(n2):
                hist += [Q] * (mF if kk else 0) + [('pA', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M2.n_out - 1))]
            hist += [Q] * (m + 1) + [('pA', (F(0),) * M2.n_out)]
            ts = len(hist) - 1
            o, _ = M2.run(hist)
            o = o[-1]
            done = False
            for cyc in range(3000):
                for sig in [Q] * 30 + [('none', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M2.n_out - 1))] * 30 + [('pA', (th,) + (F(0),) * (M2.n_out - 1))]:
                    o, _ = M2.step(o, sig)
                if all(o[0][i][0] - M2.b[i][0] <= M2.eps for i in A):
                    done = True; break
            k2n += 1
            k2ok += done
    print(f'  K3 预载构造：{k3n} 个成员（申报裕度取各自最小裕度减 1）；探针拍 T 位不夹限（命题 14-2 的前件）{k3n - k3clip} 个，其中 T 设为 b 时门控仍开启 {k3ok} 个；'
          f'探针拍 T 位夹限 {k3clip} 个，不在命题 14-2 前件内（夹限事件读 w，另论，见正文 6.4）')
    print(f'  K2 侵蚀构造：{k2n} 个成员（A₋ 取 1.25·A₊(1 − d_y)），在至多 3000 个周期内 T 各位降到 z ≤ ε 的 {k2ok} 个')
    print()

from t14_core import Lin
if __name__ == '__main__':
    print('《场具化资格必然式》主核验（run_main.py）')
    print()
    s0()
    s1()
    s2()
    s3()
    s3b()
    s4()
    s5()
    s6()
    s7()
    s8()
    print('结束。')
~~~

A.11　run\_xcheck.py　运行入口：以第十一篇判定程序逐实例交叉核对（X1—X6，分段 a—d）

~~~python
"""《场具化资格必然式》附录程序：以第十一篇 v1.0 附录 A 的判定程序（定理 D1、D2；同目录，校验值见该篇附录 B.1）
逐实例核对本篇定理 14-1⁺ 的预测与去条件见证（X1—X6）。本程序不改动第十一篇程序，只导入调用。
运行（在本目录，分段，各段独立、输出不含计时、逐字节可重现）：
  python3 run_xcheck.py a   （X1、X4、X5）
  python3 run_xcheck.py b   （X2）
  python3 run_xcheck.py c   （X3）
  python3 run_xcheck.py d   （X6）
  不带参数时依次运行全部六节（标题行注「分段 all」）。
口径：第十一篇判词 Part、HDyn 取「申报 B_FD[𝒟₁]」与「不申报」两列；判 1 不耗 B_FD（第十一篇 7.2）。
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, itertools
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
import t14_core as C
from t14_core import fr
import run_main as RM
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
import decision as DC
for f, h in RM.APPX_B.items():
    assert hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(HERE, f), 'rb').read()).hexdigest() == h, f
NONE = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))

def gate_of(M):
    return GC.Gate(M.n_in, M.n_out, M.pat, M.lam, M.theta, M.vreset, M.dx, M.dy, M.Ap, M.Am, M.rho, M.b, M.lo, M.hi)

def d2(M, hist, t0, T):
    assert M.eps == GK.EPS
    G = gate_of(M)
    I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, t0, T=[('w', i, j) for (i, j) in T])
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    j1, j0 = DC.judge(I, D, True), DC.judge(I, D, False)
    return dict(P1=j1['Part'], H1=j1['HDyn'], P0=j0['Part'], H0=j0['HDyn'], comp=all(D.complete_u.values()), vf=D.verify_fail,
                tiers=sorted({r['tier'] for r in j1['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in I.T and r['a'] == 1}))

def line(tag, r):
    return (f'{tag}{"：" if tag else ""}申报 B_FD → Part = {r["P1"]}，HDyn = {r["H1"]}；不申报 → Part = {r["P0"]}，HDyn = {r["H0"]}；'
            f'穷尽 {r["comp"]}，见证重跑失败 {r["vf"]}；T 上有效事件的 η 档位 {r["tiers"]}')

T = [(0, 0), (1, 0)]
M = C.frozen_snn_member()
def x1():
    # X1
    print('X1　实例 P（s1 冻结申报史，W = 拍 26—43）')
    r = d2(M, RM.HIST_P, 26, T)
    print('  ' + line('P', r))
    print()
def x2():
    # X2
    print('X2　冻结成员、属 𝒟 的随机轨迹 24 条（形成 = P 的 6 次配对；间奏 0—3 拍、尾 0—3 拍取 {none, A, B, AB} × I₀ ∈ {0, 1}；净探针前静置 2 拍）')
    rng = random.Random(20261106)
    letters = [(nm, (F(i0), F(0))) for nm in ['none', 'A', 'B', 'AB'] for i0 in (0, 1)]
    agree = tot = 0
    for kk in range(24):
        inter = [rng.choice(letters) for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 3))]
        tail = [rng.choice(letters) for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 3))]
        hist = RM.FORM_P + inter + [NONE, NONE, ('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + tail
        t0 = len(RM.FORM_P)
        rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, [0, 1], hist, (t0, len(hist) - 1), 6, 2, 2)
        V = C.verify(M, 0, [0, 1], hist, (t0, len(hist) - 1), 6, 2, 2, pairs=rg['pairs'])
        r = d2(M, hist, t0, T)
        tot += 1
        ok = rg['ok'] and V['R'] == 1 and r['P1'] == r['P0'] == 1 and r['H1'] == r['H0'] == 1 and r['comp'] and r['vf'] == 0
        agree += ok
        if not ok:
            print('  不一致：', [s[0] + str(s[1][0]) for s in inter + tail], line('', r))
    print(f'  属 𝒟 且本篇预测 R(T) = 1 的 {tot} 条中，第十一篇 D2 两列判 Part = 1、HDyn = 1（穷尽、见证重跑无失败）{agree} 条')
    print()
def x3():
    # X3
    print('X3　第十一篇网格 108 组中 C ∧ 制度成立的组（run_main S4）：第十一篇 D2 判词')
    n_ok = n_tot = 0
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)],
                                              [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]):
        Mg = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=W0, Ap=AP)
        form = [('A', (teach, F(0))), NONE, NONE] * n
        hist = form + [NONE] * 8 + RM.WIN0
        W = (len(form) + 8, len(hist) - 1)
        m = C.min_margin(Mg, 0, [0, 1])
        if m is None or not C.check_C(Mg, 0, [0, 1], n, 2, m)[0] or not C.check_regime(Mg, 0, [0, 1], hist, W, n, 2, m)['ok']:
            continue
        r = d2(Mg, hist, W[0], T)
        n_tot += 1
        ok = r['P1'] == r['P0'] == 1 and r['H1'] == r['H0'] == 1
        n_ok += ok
        print(f'  W0 = {fr(W0)}，A₊ = {fr(AP)}，教学 = {fr(teach)}，n = {n}：' + line('', r))
    print(f'  合计：{n_tot} 组中 D2 两列判 Part = 1、HDyn = 1 的 {n_ok} 组')
    print()
def x4():
    # X4
    print('X4　去条件见证的第十一篇判词')
    hist = RM.FORM_P + [NONE] * 3 + [('none', (F(15, 16), F(0))), NONE, ('A', (F(0), F(0))), NONE, NONE]
    r = d2(M, hist, len(hist) - 3, T)
    print('  ' + line('D-K3（m = 1，预载膜电位；W = 探针拍起 3 拍）', r))
    Mh = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=F(1, 2))
    r = d2(Mh, RM.HIST_P, 26, T)
    print('  ' + line('D-K3′（W0 = 1/2，P 型史）', r))
    form3 = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), NONE, NONE] * 3
    hist = form3 + [NONE] * 8 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + [NONE] * 5
    r = d2(M, hist, len(form3) + 8, T)
    print('  ' + line('D-F2（n = 3，单探针窗）', r))
    hist = RM.FORM_P + [NONE] * 8 + [NONE] * 6
    r = d2(M, hist, 26, T)
    print('  ' + line('D-W（W 全静置）', r))
    print('  说明：第（b）档的 0 携带 B_FD[𝒟₁]，不申报时为 ⊥_Over（第十一篇 4.2、7.2）；a = 0 或第（a）档的 0 不耗 B_FD。')
    print()
def x5():
    # X5
    print('X5　K3 第二款的必要性（run_main S3b 最小显式例：λ = 0，Σ_A b = θ，只含第一款的 K3 成立而完整 K3 不成立）')
    th = F(2)
    Ml = C.Mem(2, 1, {'none': (0, 0), 'pA': (0, 1), 'q0': (1, 0)}, [F(0)], [th], [0], [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)], F(3, 8), F(1, 16), 0,
               [[F(1, 2)], [th]], [[F(0)], [F(0)]], [[F(4)], [F(5)]])
    Q = ('none', (F(0),))
    hist = [('pA', (th,)), Q, Q, ('pA', (F(0),)), Q]
    r = d2(Ml, hist, 3, [(1, 0)])
    print('  ' + line('λ = 0 例（W = 拍 3—4）', r))
    print('  读法：若以只含第一款的 K3 代替完整 K3，定理在此成员与轨迹上会预测 R = 1，而 D2 在申报 B_FD 时判 HDyn = 0——该弱化条件被击穿（携 B_FD）；完整 K3 排除此成员。')
    print()
def x6():
    # X6
    print('X6　随机 𝒦_LS 成员（满足完整的 C，ε = 1/100，n ≤ 6）与属 𝒟 的短轨迹：第十一篇 D2 判词')
    rng = random.Random(20261107)
    got = cnt = ns_t = ns_f = ind_f = 0
    while cnt < 10:
        Mr, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        if n > 6 or mF > 2 or m > 3 or Mr.n_in * Mr.n_out > 6:
            continue
        hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, Mr, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 3), rng.randint(0, 2), gap_extra=0)
        rg = C.check_regime(Mr, 0, A, hist, (W[0], len(hist) - 1), n, mF, m)
        if not rg['ok']:
            continue
        V = C.verify(Mr, 0, A, hist, (W[0], len(hist) - 1), n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'])
        r = d2(Mr, hist, W[0], [(i, 0) for i in A])
        cnt += 1
        sep_ok = r['H1'] == 1 and r['P1'] == 1
        got += sep_ok
        if V['probe']['ns_star']:
            ns_t += 1; got_ns = sep_ok
        else:
            ns_f += 1; ind_f += r['H1'] == '⊥_Ind' and r['H0'] == '⊥_Ind'
        print(f'  成员 {cnt}：入口 {Mr.n_in}、输出 {Mr.n_out}、|A| = {len(A)}、n = {n}、史长 {len(hist)}；本篇见证 R = {V["R"]}，NS* = {V["probe"]["ns_star"]}；' + line('D2', r))
    print(f'  合计：{cnt} 个成员中 D2 判 Part = 1、HDyn = 1（申报 B_FD 列）{got} 个；NS* 成立 {ns_t} 个（预测：不耗 E11 即判 1）；'
          f'NS* 不成立 {ns_f} 个，其中 D2 两列判 HDyn = ⊥_Ind {ind_f} 个（预测：夹限两读法不一致，须申报 E11 才得 1；⊥ 不读作 0）')
    print()
PARTS = {'a': (x1, x4, x5), 'b': (x2,), 'c': (x3,), 'd': (x6,)}
if __name__ == '__main__':
    part = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'all'
    print(f'《场具化资格必然式》交叉核对（run_xcheck.py，分段 {part}）：第十一篇定理 D2 判词 vs 本篇定理 14-1⁺ 的预测')
    print()
    for fn in (PARTS[part] if part != 'all' else (x1, x2, x3, x4, x5, x6)):
        fn()
    print('结束。')
~~~

A.12　run\_source.py　运行入口：来源与无净探针窗（V1—V4）

~~~python
"""《场具化资格必然式》附录程序：来源与无净探针窗的核验（V1—V4）。只用标准库，精确有理运算；种子固定，输出确定。
运行（在本目录）：python3 run_source.py
V1　形成史前加一拍 (none, I = (0, 1))——全网络 P4R 不成立，按来源证明（F3 选定的同拍撤除，直接核 M1₀）认证 r。
V2　随机成员 × 任意其他单元输入前缀（其他列基线可为负、配对字母可含 A 外入口）：F3 的 α／β 覆盖、M1₀ 见证与 P4R 的关系；F3 不成立的显式例。
V3　两个连续 A 拍、W 只取第二拍——W 内无净探针而有 T 上事件；第十一篇 D2 判词作对照，不判负。
V4　具体无事件例：S5 D-W（W 全静置）的逐拍核对。
"""
import sys, os, random, hashlib
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
import t14_core as C
from t14_core import fr
import run_main as RM
for f, h in RM.APPX_B.items():
    assert hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(HERE, f), 'rb').read()).hexdigest() == h, f
Q = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))
FORM = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q, Q] * 6

def tev(V, A, t, j=0):
    return [(k, c) for k, c in V['recs'][t]['ev'].items() if k[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP') and k[1] in A and k[2] == j]

def v1():
    print('V1　其他单元先被注入的前缀：形成史前加一拍 (none, I = (0, 1))，其后 6 次配对、3 拍静置、一个探针；W 取探针单拍')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(); A = [0, 1]
    h = [('none', (F(0), F(1)))] + FORM + [Q] * 3 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    W = (len(h) - 1, len(h) - 1)
    okC = C.check_C(M, 0, A, 6, 2, 2)[0]
    rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, h, W, 6, 2, 2)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, A, h, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=rg['pairs'])
    rd = V['r_detail']
    o = V['pre'][rd['t']]
    print(f'  C = {okC}；制度 = {rg["ok"]}（配对拍 {rg["pairs"]}，F3 = {rg["f3"]}）；首个 T 有效写入在拍 {rd["t"]}；该拍拍前 y = {tuple(fr(q) for q in o[3])}、v = {tuple(fr(q) for q in o[1])}，P4R = {rd["P4R"]}')
    print(f'  拍前态中目标列：w_i0 = b（{all(o[0][i][0] == M.b[i][0] for i in range(4))}），x = 0（{all(q == 0 for q in o[2])}），v_0 = y_0 = 0（{o[1][0] == 0 and o[3][0] == 0}）——引理 14.3′ 的结论，不依赖其他单元')
    print(f'  F3 = {rd["mode"]}：同拍撤除 {rd["withdrawal"][0]}、I = {tuple(fr(c) for c in rd["withdrawal"][1])}（只撤 I_0，输出 1 的注入保持）；无开启 = {rd["noopen"]}；'
          f'改变的 T 有效写入 {len(rd["changed"])}/{rd["n_eff"]} ⇒ M1₀ ⇒（定理 COR（一））r = {V["r"]}')
    print(f'  q = {V["q"]}，Part = {V["part"]}，HDyn = {V["hdyn"]} ⇒ R(T) 见证 = {V["R"]}。来源只用 F3 撤除下的 M1₀，不用 P4R。')
    print()

def v2():
    print('V2　随机成员（满足 C）× 任意其他单元输入前缀：F3 覆盖、M1₀ 见证与 P4R')
    rng = random.Random(20261108)
    cnt = dict(traj=0, a=0, b=0, none=0, r=0, r_f3=0, p4r_false=0, p4r_false_r=0, R=0)
    for _ in range(250):
        M0, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        b = [row[:] for row in M0.b]; lo = [row[:] for row in M0.lo]; hi = [row[:] for row in M0.hi]
        for i in range(M0.n_in):
            for k in range(1, M0.n_out):
                lo[i][k] = F(-2); b[i][k] = F(rng.randint(-8, 8), 8); hi[i][k] = F(2)
        M = C.Mem(M0.n_in, M0.n_out, M0.pat, M0.lam, M0.theta, M0.vreset, M0.dx, M0.dy, M0.Ap, M0.Am, 0, b, lo, hi, M0.eps)
        assert C.check_C(M, 0, A, n, mF, m)[0]
        for _ in range(4):
            hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 6), rng.randint(0, 6))
            pre = [('none', tuple(F(0) if k == 0 else F(rng.randint(0, 8), 4) * M.theta[0] for k in range(M.n_out)))
                   for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3))]
            hist = pre + hist; W = (W[0] + len(pre), W[1] + len(pre)); tp += len(pre)
            rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
            V = C.verify(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, pairs=rg['pairs'], probe=tp)
            rd = V['r_detail']
            cnt['traj'] += 1
            md = rg['f3']
            cnt['a' if md == 'α' else 'b' if md == 'β' else 'none'] += 1
            cnt['r'] += V['r'] == 1
            if md is not None:
                cnt['r_f3'] += V['r'] == 1
            if not rd['P4R']:
                cnt['p4r_false'] += 1; cnt['p4r_false_r'] += V['r'] == 1
            cnt['R'] += V['R'] == 1
    print(f'  轨迹 {cnt["traj"]} 条（每条前加 1—3 拍只向其他输出注入的前缀；其他列基线取 [−1, 1]）：F3 = α {cnt["a"]}、β {cnt["b"]}、不成立 {cnt["none"]}；')
    print(f'  F3 成立的 {cnt["a"] + cnt["b"]} 条中 M1₀ 见证 r = 1 共 {cnt["r_f3"]} 条；P4R 不成立 {cnt["p4r_false"]} 条，其中 r = 1 {cnt["p4r_false_r"]} 条；属 𝒟 且 R(T) 见证成立 {cnt["R"]} 条')
    # F3 不成立的显式例：配对字母 AB 使目标列基线和 = θ（α 不成立），输出 1 的列基线和为负（β 不成立）
    pat = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}
    b = [[F(1, 4), F(-1, 2)] for _ in range(4)]
    lo = [[F(0), F(-2)] for _ in range(4)]
    hi = [[F(2), F(2)] for _ in range(4)]
    M = C.Mem(4, 2, pat, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [1, 1], [0, 0], [F(1, 2)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), F(1, 16), 0, b, lo, hi)
    okC = C.check_C(M, 0, [0, 1], 6, 2, 2)[0]
    h = [('none', (F(0), F(3, 4)))] + [('AB', (F(1), F(3, 4))), Q, Q] * 6 + [Q] * 3 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    W = (len(h) - 1, len(h) - 1)
    rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, [0, 1], h, W, 6, 2, 2)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, [0, 1], h, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=rg['pairs'])
    o = V['pre'][1]
    quiet_all = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))
    _, rq = M.step(o, quiet_all); _, ra = M.step(o, h[1])
    _, rb = M.step(o, ('none', (F(0), F(3, 4))))
    print(f'  F3 不成立例：系数部分 C = {okC}；制度 = {rg["ok"]}（{"；".join(rg["why"])}）；首配对拍 1 上输出 1：实际 vt = {fr(ra["vt"][1])} 不发放；'
          f'β 型撤除（入口撤为 none、保留 I_1）后 vt = {fr(rb["vt"][1])} 发放（新开启，撤除不容许）；全撤至静置字母后 vt = {fr(rq["vt"][1])}、发放 {rq["g"][1]}')
    print(f'    本篇判据不出 r 见证（r = {V["r"]}），按第十一篇 4.3 记 ⊥ 而非 0；此例在 C 之外（F3 是 C_init 的一项）。')
    print()

def v3():
    print('V3　无净探针的窗：形成史后 3 拍静置、两个连续 A 拍，W 只取第二个 A 拍（m = 2）')
    import gate_class as GC, gate_criteria as GK, decision as DC
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(); A = [0, 1]
    h = FORM + [Q] * 3 + [('A', (F(0), F(0))), ('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    W = (len(h) - 1, len(h) - 1)
    rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, h, W, 6, 2, 2)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, A, h, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=rg['pairs'])
    ev = tev(V, A, W[0])
    pb = V['probe']
    print(f'  净探针拍 {rg["probes"]}（制度 R2 不成立：{"；".join(rg["why"])}）；W 内作用于 T 的事件 {len(ev)} 个：' +
          '，'.join(f'{k[0]}{k[1]}{k[2]} = {fr(c)}' for k, c in ev))
    print(f'  该拍（非净探针）拍前 v_0 = {fr(V["pre"][W[0]][1][0])}；实际 vt = {fr(pb["vt"])} 发放；T 设为 b 后 vt = {fr(pb["vt_cf"])}，发放 {pb["g_cf"]}；本程序见证 R = {V["R"]}')
    G = GC.Gate(M.n_in, M.n_out, M.pat, M.lam, M.theta, M.vreset, M.dx, M.dy, M.Ap, M.Am, M.rho, M.b, M.lo, M.hi)
    I = GK.Instance('', G, h, W[0])
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    j1, j0 = DC.judge(I, D, True), DC.judge(I, D, False)
    print(f'  第十一篇 D2：申报 B_FD → Part = {j1["Part"]}，HDyn = {j1["HDyn"]}；不申报 → Part = {j0["Part"]}，HDyn = {j0["HDyn"]}；穷尽 {all(D.complete_u.values())}，见证重跑失败 {D.verify_fail}')
    print('  读法：W 内无净探针只表示净探针见证不适用（此窗另有证书探针，属定理 14-1⁺，见 run_ext D1），不推出无事件，也不推出资格失败；此例作为对照保留，不判负（同正文 8.5 的无净探针窗；此例有证书探针，见 run_ext D1）。')
    print()

def v4():
    print('V4　具体无事件例（S5 D-W，只就此具体史陈述）：P 的形成史 + 8 拍静置 + W = 6 拍静置')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(); A = [0, 1]
    h = FORM + [Q] * 8 + [Q] * 6
    pre, recs = M.run(h)
    W = (26, 31)
    nT = sum(len([k for k in recs[t]['ev'] if k[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP') and k[1] in A and k[2] == 0]) for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1))
    fired = [t for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1) if recs[t]['g'][0]]
    print(f'  W 内门控 0 发放拍 {fired}；作用于 T 的事件 {nT} 个。证明：W 内无入口（无 MINUS）、I_0 = 0 且 v_0 < θ（引理 14.1、14.2，不发放，无 PLUS）、ρ = 0（无 RELAX）、w 在 [lo, hi] 内不变（无 CLIP）。')
    print()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print('《场具化资格必然式》来源与无净探针窗（run_source.py）')
    print()
    v1(); v2(); v3(); v4()
    print('结束。')
~~~

A.13　run\_ext.py　运行入口：扩展核验（D0—D8）

~~~python
"""《场具化资格必然式》附录程序：扩展核验 D0—D8（正文第五、六节）。只用标准库，精确有理运算；种子固定，输出确定。
运行（在本目录）：python3 run_ext.py
程序给出的是有限核验、显式见证与反例；全称结论以正文的证明为准。
"""
import sys, os, hashlib, random, itertools
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
import t14_core as C
import t14_ext as X
from t14_core import fr
import run_main as RM
T13_SHA = {'t13_gate.py': '1d60bf21f93ab080cc2254ad92e0539612a8706f919487d681845f4fef66b90d'}
Q2 = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))
FORM = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * 6
A01 = [0, 1]

def d2(M, hist, t0, T):
    import gate_class as GC, gate_criteria as GK, decision as DC
    G = GC.Gate(M.n_in, M.n_out, M.pat, M.lam, M.theta, M.vreset, M.dx, M.dy, M.Ap, M.Am, M.rho, M.b, M.lo, M.hi)
    I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, t0, T=[('w', i, j) for (i, j) in T])
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    j1, j0 = DC.judge(I, D, True), DC.judge(I, D, False)
    return dict(P1=j1['Part'], H1=j1['HDyn'], P0=j0['Part'], H0=j0['HDyn'], hs=j1['HDyn_sep'], hj=j1['HDyn_joint'],
                comp=all(D.complete_u.values()), vf=D.verify_fail)

def d0():
    print('D0　版本绑定；𝒦_PA^≺ 三门控设计的独立实现与第十三篇附录 t13_gate.step 逐拍对照')
    for f, h in RM.APPX_B.items():
        assert hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(HERE, f), 'rb').read()).hexdigest() == h
    for f, h in T13_SHA.items():
        got = hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(HERE, f), 'rb').read()).hexdigest()
        assert got == h
        print(f'  {f}  SHA-256 {got[:16]}…  与第十三篇 v1.0 附录 B.1 相符；第十一篇六文件同 run_main S0')
    import t13_gate as TG
    rng = random.Random(20261201)
    bad = n = 0
    for _ in range(200):
        P = rand_pa(rng, need=False)
        st = P.init(); st13 = (st[0], st[1], st[2], st[3], [0, 0, 0])
        Pt = P.as_t13()
        for _ in range(30):
            nm = rng.choice(list(P.pat)); I = (F(rng.randint(0, 4), 2), F(rng.randint(0, 4), 2), F(0))
            st, r = P.step(st, (nm, I))
            st13, g, vt, ev = TG.step(Pt, st13, P.pat[nm], list(I))
            n += 1
            if st[0] != st13[0] or st[1] != st13[1] or st[2] != st13[2] or st[3] != st13[3] or [int(q) for q in g] != r['g']:
                bad += 1
            for k, c in r['ev'].items():
                if k[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'CLIP') and ev[k] != (True, c):
                    bad += 1
    print(f'  随机 PA3 成员 200 个 × 30 拍 = {n} 拍：本篇 PA3.step 与第十三篇 t13_gate.step（mode = dag）全状态、门控与 PLUS/MINUS/CLIP 分项不一致 {bad}')
    print()

def d1():
    print('D1　定理 14-1⁺：证书探针（引理 14.7）代替净探针——无净探针与多探针窗')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    rng = random.Random(20261031)
    cls = dict(noA=0, clean=0, cert_only=0, uncert=0)
    certW = []
    for kk in range(200):
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'none', 'none']), (F(0), F(0))) for _ in range(18)]
        hist = FORM + [Q2] * 8 + win
        W = (26, 43)
        rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2)
        cp = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 15)
        if not any(s[0] == 'A' for s in win):
            cls['noA'] += 1
        elif rg['ok']:
            cls['clean'] += 1
        elif cp:
            cls['cert_only'] += 1; certW.append((hist, W, cp))
        else:
            cls['uncert'] += 1
    print(f'  第十一篇随机输入族 200 条（种子 20261031）：无 A {cls["noA"]}；有净探针 {cls["clean"]}；无净探针而有证书探针 {cls["cert_only"]}；有 A 而无证书探针 {cls["uncert"]}')
    okc = 0
    for hist, W, cp in certW:
        V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=[3 * k for k in range(6)], probe=cp[0])
        okc += V['R'] == 1
    print(f'    无净探针而有证书探针的 {len(certW)} 条：在首个证书探针上本程序 R 见证成立 {okc} 条')
    xs = certW[:4]
    for hist, W, cp in xs:
        r = d2(M, hist, W[0], [(0, 0), (1, 0)])
        print(f'    第十一篇 D2（证书探针 {cp}）：申报 B_FD → Part = {r["P1"]}，HDyn = {r["H1"]}；不申报 → HDyn = {r["H0"]}；穷尽 {r["comp"]}，重跑失败 {r["vf"]}')
    # 两个连续 A 的窗
    h = FORM + [Q2] * 3 + [('A', (F(0), F(0))), ('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    cp = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, h, (len(h) - 1, len(h) - 1), 15)
    print(f'  两个连续 A、W 取第二拍（run_source V3）：证书探针 {cp}（前一拍含 A，U = 0；Σ_A b = 1/2 < θ）——落入定理 14-1⁺ 的覆盖')
    # 随机成员 × 无静置要求的随机窗
    rng = random.Random(20261202)
    tot = cert = Rok = unc = 0
    for _ in range(300):
        Mr, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        for _ in range(4):
            hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, Mr, 0, A, n, mF, 0, rng.randint(0, 12), rng.randint(0, 12))
            pn = C.check_regime(Mr, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, 0)['pairs'][-1]
            cp = X.certified_probes(Mr, 0, A, hist, W, pn)
            tot += 1
            if cp:
                cert += 1
                V = C.verify(Mr, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, 0, probe=cp[0])
                Rok += V['R'] == 1 and V['hdyn'] == 1
            else:
                unc += 1
    print(f'  随机 𝒦_LS 成员 300 × 4 条（探针前不强加静置）：有证书探针 {cert} 条，其上 R 见证成立 {Rok} 条；无证书探针 {unc} 条（不判）')
    # 冻结成员穷尽：间奏 3 拍 + 探针 + 尾 2 拍，无静置要求
    letters = [(nm, (F(i0), F(0))) for nm in ['none', 'A', 'B', 'AB'] for i0 in (0, 1)]
    c1 = c2 = c3 = c4 = 0
    for combo in itertools.product(letters, repeat=5):
        hist = FORM + list(combo[:3]) + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))] + list(combo[3:])
        W = (18, len(hist) - 1)
        cp = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 15)
        if cp:
            c1 += 1
            V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2, probe=cp[0])
            c2 += V['R'] == 1
        else:
            c3 += 1
            V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2)
            c4 += V['R'] == 1
    print(f'  冻结成员穷尽（形成后间奏 3 拍、固定探针、尾 2 拍，8^5 = 32768 条）：有证书探针 {c1} 条，R 见证 {c2}；'
          f'无证书探针 {c3} 条，其中本程序仍在别的探针拍得 R 见证 {c4} 条（定理之外，不判）')
    print()

def d2_sat():
    print('D2　夹限与 E11（命题 14-4）：窗首饱和时联合读法下 W 内 T 上无有效联合作用')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(wmax=F(7, 10))
    hist = FORM + ([('A', (F(0), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * 6) + [Q2] * 2 + [('A', (F(0), F(0))), Q2, Q2, ('AB', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2, ('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    ts = 18 + 18 + 2
    W = (ts, len(hist) - 1)
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, 0, A01, 6, 2, 2)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=[3 * k for k in range(6)])
    gap = [M.hi[i][0] - V['pre'][ts][0][i][0] for i in A01]
    nets = X.joint_nets(V, M, 0, A01, W)
    sep_eff = [k for t in range(W[0], W[1] + 1) for k, c in V['recs'][t]['ev'].items() if k[0] == 'PLUS' and k[1] in A01 and k[2] == 0 and abs(c) > M.eps]
    print(f'  冻结成员取 hi = 7/10：C = {ok}；窗首 hi − w = {[fr(g) for g in gap]}（≤ ε）；W 内 T 位联合净增量最大 {fr(max(nets.values()))}；分列读法下有效 PLUS {len(sep_eff)} 个；本程序（分列）R 见证 = {V["R"]}，NS* = {V["probe"]["ns_star"]}')
    r = d2(M, hist, W[0], [(0, 0), (1, 0)])
    print(f'  第十一篇 D2：分列 HDyn = {r["hs"]}，联合 HDyn = {r["hj"]} ⇒ 判词 {r["H1"]}（穷尽 {r["comp"]}）——E11 在此不可省；联合读法下净变化为零的判据属第一篇第八节未答项')
    # 随机：窗首饱和 ⇒ 联合净增量 ≤ ε；NS* 成立 ⇒ 联合读法亦得见证
    rng = random.Random(20261203)
    satn = viol = nsok = nsn = 0
    for _ in range(300):
        Mr, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        for _ in range(4):
            hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, Mr, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 30), rng.randint(0, 10))
            V = C.verify(Mr, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m, probe=tp)
            if all(Mr.hi[i][0] - V['pre'][W[0]][0][i][0] <= Mr.eps for i in A):
                satn += 1
                viol += any(v > Mr.eps for v in X.joint_nets(V, Mr, 0, A, W).values())
            if V['probe']['ns_star']:
                nsn += 1
                o = V['pre'][tp]; rr = V['recs'][tp]
                good = False
                for i in A:
                    net = V['pre'][tp + 1][0][i][0] - o[0][i][0]
                    clip = ('CLIP', i, 0) in rr['ev']
                    if (not clip and abs(rr['ev'].get(('PLUS', i, 0), F(0))) > Mr.eps) or (clip and net > Mr.eps):
                        good = True
                nsok += good
    print(f'  随机 300 成员 × 4 条：窗首饱和 {satn} 条，其中 W 内出现有效联合净增量 {viol} 条；NS* 成立 {nsn} 条，其中探针拍有联合读法下的有效 T 作用（不夹限的有效 PLUS 或夹限而净增量 > ε）{nsok} 条')
    print()

def d3():
    print('D3　来源：F3 的两种放宽 F3*、F3°（命题 14-5（一））与 F3ᵤ 之外的阻断构造（命题 14-5（二））')
    rng = random.Random(20261204)
    tot = f3 = f3s = f3d = fu = ok_u = 0
    blk = blk_ok = rest = 0
    for _ in range(2000):
        n_in = rng.choice([2, 3, 4]); n_out = rng.choice([2, 3])
        A = sorted(rng.sample(range(n_in), rng.randint(1, n_in - 1)))
        pats = {'none': (0,) * n_in, 'pA': tuple(1 if i in A else 0 for i in range(n_in))}
        for kk in range(rng.randint(1, 4)):
            wa = rng.choice([0, 1])
            pats[f'q{kk}'] = tuple(wa if i in A else rng.choice([0, 1]) for i in range(n_in))
        th = [F(1)] + [F(rng.randint(1, 4), 2) for _ in range(n_out - 1)]
        vres = [F(0)] + [rng.choice([F(0), th[k], 2 * th[k]]) for k in range(1, n_out)]
        b = [[F(rng.randint(0, 6), 8)] + [F(rng.randint(-12, 6), 8) for _ in range(n_out - 1)] for _ in range(n_in)]
        lo = [[F(0)] + [F(-3)] * (n_out - 1) for _ in range(n_in)]
        hi = [[F(3)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        lam = [F(rng.randint(1, 8), 8) for _ in range(n_out)]
        M = C.Mem(n_in, n_out, pats, lam, th, vres, [F(1, 2)] * n_in, [F(1, 2)] * n_out, F(1, 8), F(1, 16), 0, b, lo, hi)
        withA = [nm for nm, p in pats.items() if all(p[i] for i in A)]
        a1 = rng.choice(withA)
        sig = (a1, tuple(th[0] if k == 0 else F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2) for k in range(n_out)))
        tot += 1
        c3 = C.f3_mode(M, 0, sig) is not None
        cs = bool(X.f3star(M, 0, A, sig))
        cd = X.f3deg(M, 0)
        f3 += c3; f3s += cs; f3d += cd
        if c3 or cs or cd:
            fu += 1
            # 任意其他单元前缀（1—3 拍，只向其他单元注入，可使其发放）之后，在首配对拍核：存在满足 M1₀ 的同拍容许撤除
            o = M.init()
            for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3)):
                o, _r = M.step(o, ('none', tuple(F(0) if k == 0 else F(rng.randint(0, 6), 2) for k in range(n_out))))
            ws, hits = X.m10_any(M, 0, A, o, sig, M.eps)
            ok_u += bool(ws)
        else:
            k = X.blocker2(M, 0, A, sig)
            if k is None:
                rest += 1
                continue
            pre = ('none', tuple(F(0) if kk != k else M.theta[k] + 1 for kk in range(n_out)))
            sig2 = (a1, tuple(th[0] if kk == 0 else F(0) for kk in range(n_out)))
            o, r0 = M.step(M.init(), pre)
            ws, hits = X.m10_any(M, 0, A, o, sig2, M.eps)
            blk += 1
            blk_ok += r0['g'][k] == 1 and not ws
    print(f'  随机成员 {tot} 个（其他单元：θ_k ∈ {{1/2, 1, 3/2, 2}}，v_reset,k ∈ {{0, θ_k, 2θ_k}}，其他列基线可负；字母表 1—4 个 A-不可拆字母）：')
    print(f'    F3 成立 {f3}；F3* 成立 {f3s}；F3° 成立 {f3d}；F3ᵤ = F3 ∨ F3* ∨ F3° 成立 {fu}；其中任意其他单元前缀后首配对拍存在满足 M1₀ 的同拍容许撤除 {ok_u}/{fu}')
    print(f'    F3ᵤ 不成立而阻断条件成立 {blk} 个：前缀一拍使阻断单元 k 发放（v_k = v_reset,k），首配对拍上穷举全部同拍容许撤除，无一满足 M1₀ 的 {blk_ok} 个；两者都不成立 {rest} 个（不判，登记）')
    # 显式例
    pats = {'none': (0, 0), 'pA': (1, 0), 'AB': (1, 1)}
    M = C.Mem(2, 2, pats, [F(1, 2), F(1)], [F(1), F(1)], [F(0), F(2)], [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), F(1, 16), 0,
              [[F(1, 2), F(-1, 2)], [F(3, 4), F(-3, 4)]], [[F(0), F(-3)], [F(0), F(-3)]], [[F(3), F(3)], [F(3), F(3)]])
    sig = ('AB', (F(1), F(0)))
    o, r0 = M.step(M.init(), ('none', (F(0), F(2))))
    ws, hits = X.m10_any(M, 0, [0], o, sig, M.eps)
    print(f'  显式例：A = {{入口 0}}，首配对字母 AB（目标列基线和 5/4 ≥ θ，α 不成立）；输出 1：v_reset = 2，λ = 1，列基线 (−1/2, −3/4)；'
          f'F3 = {C.f3_mode(M, 0, sig)}，F3* = {X.f3star(M, 0, [0], sig)}，F3° = {X.f3deg(M, 0)}，阻断单元 = {X.blocker2(M, 0, [0], sig)}；'
          f'前缀使输出 1 发放后，首配对拍 T 有效写入 {len(hits)} 个，满足 M1₀ 的同拍撤除 {len(ws)} 个')
    print()

def d4():
    print('D4　形成阈（命题 14-6）：确定性形成结果 Φ(g)、改进界 F2′ 与精确性')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    rows = []
    for n in range(1, 9):
        w, incs = X.formation_outcome(M, 0, A01, [2] * (n - 1), n)
        hist = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * n
        pre, recs = M.run(hist)
        sim = pre[3 * (n - 1) + 1][0][0][0]
        phi = sum(w.values())
        f2 = sum(min(M.hi[i][0], M.b[i][0] + n * (M.Ap - M.Am / (1 - M.dy[0] ** 3))) for i in A01)
        f2p, _ = X.f2prime(M, 0, A01, n, 2)
        rows.append((n, phi, f2p, f2, sim == w[0]))
    print('  冻结成员、等间隔 m_F = 2：n | Φ = Σ_A w(p_n+1) | F2′ 界 | F2 界 | 与 Mem 运行一致')
    for n, phi, f2p, f2, same in rows:
        print(f'    {n} | {fr(phi)} | {fr(f2p)} | {fr(f2)} | {same}')
    print('  门槛 θ = 1：Φ 首次 ≥ 1 在 n = 4；F2′ 在 n = ' + str(next(n for n, p, q, r, s in rows if q >= 1)) + '；F2 在 n = ' + str(next(n for n, p, q, r, s in rows if r >= 1)))
    # 必要性构造：Φ < θ 时 单探针长静置窗
    form3 = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * 3
    for L in (8, 20):
        hist = form3 + [Q2] * L + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
        W = (len(hist) - 1, len(hist) - 1)
        V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 3, 2, 2)
        evs = [(k, c) for k, c in V['recs'][W[0]]['ev'].items() if k[0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'CLIP', 'RELAX') and k[1] in A01 and k[2] == 0]
        print(f'  n = 3（Φ = {fr(rows[2][1])} < θ），静置 {L} 拍后单探针窗：门控 {V["recs"][W[0]]["g"][0]}；T 上事件 ' +
              '，'.join(f'{k[0]}{k[1]}0 = {fr(c)}' for k, c in evs) + f'（|c| > ε 的 {sum(abs(c) > M.eps for k, c in evs)} 个）')
    hist = form3 + [Q2] * 8 + [('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    r = d2(M, hist, len(hist) - 1, [(0, 0), (1, 0)])
    print(f'    第十一篇 D2（静置 8 拍）：HDyn = {r["H1"]}（申报 B_FD）/ {r["H0"]}（不申报）——a = 0，不耗 B_FD')
    # 随机：F2 ≤ F2′ ≤ Φ 的次序；Φ ≥ θ 而 F2 不成立的成员上 R 见证
    rng = random.Random(20261205)
    order_bad = cnt = gain = Rgain = 0
    for _ in range(400):
        Mr, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        for _ in range(3):
            nn = max(1, n - rng.randint(0, 3))
            gaps = [mF + rng.randint(0, 3) for _ in range(nn - 1)]
            w, incs = X.formation_outcome(Mr, 0, A, gaps, nn)
            phi = sum(w.values())
            f2p, _ = X.f2prime(Mr, 0, A, nn, mF)
            dF = Mr.Ap - Mr.Am / (1 - Mr.dy[0] ** (mF + 1))
            f2 = sum(min(Mr.hi[i][0], Mr.b[i][0] + nn * dF) for i in A)
            cnt += 1
            order_bad += not (f2 <= f2p <= phi)
            if phi >= Mr.theta[0] and f2 < Mr.theta[0] and min(w[i] - Mr.b[i][0] for i in A) > Mr.eps:
                gain += 1
                th = Mr.theta[0]; no = Mr.n_out
                Qr = ('none', (F(0),) * no)
                hist = []
                for k in range(nn):
                    if k:
                        hist += [Qr] * gaps[k - 1]
                    hist.append(('pA', (th,) + (F(0),) * (no - 1)))
                pn = len(hist) - 1
                for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 10)):
                    hist.append((rng.choice(list(Mr.pat)), tuple(rng.choice([F(0), th]) for _ in range(no))))
                hist += [Qr] * m + [('pA', (F(0),) * no)]
                tp = len(hist) - 1
                V = C.verify(Mr, 0, A, hist, (pn + 1, tp), nn, mF, m, probe=tp)
                Rgain += V['R'] == 1
    print(f'  随机成员 400 × 3（随机间隔 ≥ m_F）：F2 ≤ F2′ ≤ Φ 次序违例 {order_bad}/{cnt}；Φ ≥ θ 而 F2 不成立 {gain} 例，其上（以 Φ 代 F2 的定理 14-1⁺）R 见证成立 {Rgain} 例')
    print()

def d5():
    print('D5　K2′ 与边界：A₋ = 0（d_y 可为 1）、λ = 1（Σ_A b = 0）；d_x = 1 时 K2 失效而侵蚀构造的表现（登记）')
    rng = random.Random(20261206)
    a = b_ = c_ = d_ = 0
    for _ in range(200):
        M0, A, n, mF, m = RM.rand_member(rng)
        M = C.Mem(M0.n_in, M0.n_out, M0.pat, M0.lam, M0.theta, M0.vreset, M0.dx, [F(1)] * M0.n_out, M0.Ap, F(0), 0, M0.b, M0.lo, M0.hi, M0.eps)
        hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, n, 0, m, rng.randint(0, 30), rng.randint(0, 30))
        pn = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, 0, m)['pairs'][-1]
        pre, recs = M.run(hist)
        a += 1
        b_ += all(pre[t + 1][0][i][0] >= pre[t][0][i][0] for t in range(pn + 1, len(hist)) for i in A)
    print(f'  A₋ = 0、d_y = 1（K2 原式不成立、K2′ 成立）：200 条随机轨迹中形成后 T 位单调不减 {b_}/{a}')
    for _ in range(200):
        n_in = 2; A = [0, 1]
        pat = {'none': (0, 0), 'pA': (1, 1)}
        M = C.Mem(2, 1, pat, [F(1)], [F(1)], [0], [F(1, 2)] * 2, [F(1, 2)], F(1, 4), F(1, 16), 0, [[F(0)], [F(0)]], [[F(0)], [F(0)]], [[F(3)], [F(3)]])
        hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, 4, 2, 0, rng.randint(0, 10), rng.randint(0, 5))
        V = C.verify(M, 0, A, hist, W, 4, 2, 0, probe=tp)
        c_ += 1; d_ += V['R'] == 1
    okC = C.check_C(M, 0, A, 4, 2, 0)[0]
    print(f'  λ = 1、Σ_A b = 0（K3(0) 成立：C = {okC}）：200 条随机轨迹 R 见证 {d_}/{c_}')
    # d_x = 1：K2 不成立的侵蚀构造
    M = C.Mem(4, 2, {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}, [F(3, 4)] * 2, [1, 1], [0, 0],
              [F(1)] * 4, [F(1, 2)] * 2, F(1, 8), F(1, 12), 0, [[F(1, 4)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(0)] * 2 for _ in range(4)], [[F(2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)])
    hist, ts = RM.erosion_hist(M, A01, 9, 2, K1=12, K2=12, cycles=200)
    pre, recs = M.run(hist)
    zmin = min(min(o[0][i][0] - M.b[i][0] for i in A01) for o in pre[ts:])
    dec = sum(1 for t in range(ts, len(hist)) if any(pre[t + 1][0][i][0] < pre[t][0][i][0] for i in A01))
    print(f'  d_x = 1、A₋ = 1/12（K2 不成立）：命题 14-3 的侵蚀构造 200 周期后 min z = {fr(zmin)}，T 位下降拍 {dec}——资格迹 x 不衰减时侵蚀被增强压过，命题 14-3 的 d_x < 1 前件不可删（K2 在 d_x = 1 时的精确性登记待证）')
    print()

def d6():
    print('D6　随机输入制度（命题 14-8；读法：形成后各拍字母独立同分布，先申报分布 π，再报概率下界）')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    pi = [(('none', (F(0), F(0))), F(1, 2)), (('A', (F(0), F(0))), F(1, 8)), (('B', (F(0), F(0))), F(1, 8)),
          (('AB', (F(0), F(0))), F(1, 16)), (('none', (F(1), F(0))), F(1, 16)), (('A', (F(1), F(0))), F(1, 16)), (('B', (F(1), F(0))), F(1, 16))]
    assert sum(p for _, p in pi) == 1
    pq = F(1, 2); pp = F(1, 8)
    rng = random.Random(20261207)
    cum = []
    acc = F(0)
    for sig, p in pi:
        acc += p; cum.append((acc, sig))
    def draw():
        u = F(rng.randint(0, 2 ** 20 - 1), 2 ** 20)
        for c, sig in cum:
            if u < c:
                return sig
    print('  π：(none,0) 1/2，(A,0) 1/8，(B,0) 1/8，(AB,0) 1/16，(none,I₀=1) 1/16，(A,I₀=1) 1/16，(B,I₀=1) 1/16；m = 2')
    for L in (6, 12, 24, 48):
        N = 1000
        clean = cert = Rw = 0
        for _ in range(N):
            win = [draw() for _ in range(L)]
            hist = FORM + win
            W = (18, len(hist) - 1)
            rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2)
            cp = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 15)
            clean += rg['ok']; cert += bool(cp)
            if cp:
                V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, hist, W, 6, 2, 2, probe=cp[0])
                Rw += V['R'] == 1
        lb = X.iid_lower_bound(pq, pp, 2, L)
        print(f'    L = {L}：下界 1 − (1 − pq²·pp)^⌊L/3⌋ = {fr(lb)}；样本 {N}：含净探针 {clean}，含证书探针 {cert}，证书探针上 R 见证 {Rw}')
    print('  读法：R 的全称部分（不变量、留存、枢轴）是路径全称的；随机性只进入「W 含证书探针」这一事件的概率。概率 1 版本：无限长后续中证书探针几乎必然出现无穷多次。')
    print()

def rand_pa(rng, need=True):
    while True:
        n_in = rng.choice([2, 3, 4])
        A = sorted(rng.sample(range(n_in), rng.randint(1, n_in - 1)))
        pats = {'none': (0,) * n_in, 'pA': tuple(1 if i in A else 0 for i in range(n_in))}
        for kk in range(rng.randint(0, 2)):
            wa = rng.choice([0, 1]); pats[f'q{kk}'] = tuple(wa if i in A else rng.choice([0, 1]) for i in range(n_in))
        lam = [F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8) for _ in range(3)]
        th = [F(1), F(rng.randint(1, 4), 4), F(rng.randint(1, 8), 2)]
        dx = [F(rng.randint(0, 6), 8) for _ in range(n_in)]
        dy = [F(rng.randint(0, 6), 8) for _ in range(3)]
        Am = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 32)
        Ap = max(Am / (1 - dy[1]), F(1, 100)) + F(rng.randint(1, 4), 32)
        m = rng.randint(0, 4)
        room = th[0] - lam[0] ** (m + 1) * th[0]
        if room <= 0:
            continue
        bM = [room / len(A) * F(rng.randint(1, 7), 8) if i in A else F(rng.randint(0, 4), 8) for i in range(n_in)]
        u = th[1] + F(rng.randint(0, 4), 8) if need else F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8)
        P = X.PA3(n_in, pats, lam, th, dx, dy, Ap, Am, u, bM, F(3))
        P.A, P.m = A, m
        if not need:
            return P
        for n in range(1, 300):
            ok, items = X.check_C_PA(P, A, n, 1, m)
            if ok:
                P.n = n
                return P

def pa_traj(rng, P, inter, tail):
    A, n, m = P.A, P.n, P.m
    Q = ('none', (F(0), F(0), F(0)))
    hist = []
    for k in range(n):
        hist += [Q] * (1 + rng.randint(0, 2) if k else rng.randint(0, 1))
        hist.append(('pA', (F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2), P.theta[1] + F(rng.randint(0, 2), 4), F(0))))
    pn = len(hist) - 1
    for _ in range(inter):
        hist.append((rng.choice(list(P.pat)), (rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)]), rng.choice([F(0), P.theta[1]]), F(0))))
    hist += [Q] * m + [('pA', (F(0), F(0), F(0)))]
    tp = len(hist) - 1
    for _ in range(tail):
        hist.append((rng.choice(list(P.pat)), (rng.choice([F(0), F(1)]), rng.choice([F(0), P.theta[1]]), F(0))))
    return hist, pn, tp

def d7():
    print('D7　门控扩展类 𝒦_PA^≺（第十三篇 v1.0 定义 5、7；PA-2 闭合）：三门控设计 M ≺ D（Z 永不发放）上的 FD 级 C_PA ⇒ R_FD（定理 14-9）')
    rng = random.Random(20261208)
    tot = ok = inv = zf = sat = 0
    for _ in range(250):
        P = rand_pa(rng)
        for _ in range(4):
            hist, pn, tp = pa_traj(rng, P, rng.randint(0, 25), rng.randint(0, 15))
            Wt = (pn + 1, len(hist) - 1)
            res = X.pa_witness(P, P.A, hist, Wt, tp)
            pre = res['pre']
            mono = all(pre[t + 1][0][i][0] >= pre[t][0][i][0] for t in range(pn + 1, len(hist)) for i in P.A)
            # （六）首配对拍：撤至静置字母 (none, 0, 0, 0) 无开启且 PLUS_iM 不发生
            p1 = next(t for t, sg in enumerate(hist) if sg[0] == 'pA' and sg[1][1] >= P.theta[1])
            o1 = pre[p1]; r1 = res['recs'][p1]
            _, rq = P.step(o1, ('none', (F(0), F(0), F(0))))
            six = all(rq['g'][k] <= r1['g'][k] for k in range(3)) and any(('PLUS', i, 0) in r1['ev'] and abs(r1['ev'][('PLUS', i, 0)]) > P.eps
                                                                          and ('PLUS', i, 0) not in rq['ev'] for i in P.A)
            tot += 1
            ok += bool(res['eff']) and bool(res['fd']) and res['path'] and res['q'] and not res['zfire'] and six
            inv += not mono; zf += res['zfire']; sat += bool(res['sat'])
    print(f'  随机 PA3 成员 250 个（满足 C_PA）× 4 条轨迹：探针拍 PLUS_iM 有效、T 设为 b 后 M 与 D 都不发放而 PLUS_iM 不发生、窗末留存、Z 不发放、首配对拍撤至静置字母无开启且 PLUS_iM 不发生，全部成立 {ok}/{tot}；'
          f'形成后 T 位下降 {inv}；Z 发放 {zf}；探针拍夹限 {sat}')
    # 对照：PD1 不成立（u < θ_D）
    rng = random.Random(20261209)
    c1 = c1ok = 0
    for _ in range(100):
        P = rand_pa(rng)
        P.u = P.theta[1] / 2
        hist, pn, tp = pa_traj(rng, P, 0, 0)
        res = X.pa_witness(P, P.A, hist, (pn + 1, tp), tp)
        c1 += 1; c1ok += bool(res['fd'])
    print(f'  对照 PD1 不成立（u = θ_D/2）：100 条单探针窗中探针拍得到 FD 见证 {c1ok} 条（M 发放而 D 不发放，PLUS_iM 本不发生）')
    print('  强度：结论为 FD 级——有效作用、𝒟₁ 函数依赖见证、实际事件图路径（T → SYN → M → FIN(M,D) → D → PLUS_iM）、窗末留存；')
    print('       读回 HistCond 须 𝒦_PA^≺ 上的局部对应引理（第十三篇 U13-16、第十一篇 U8，未证），r 须判据 PS 在扩展类上的对应（未证）。')
    print()

def d8():
    print('D8　族一ᵍ：𝒦_LS 的非神经读法（阈值冲刷池网），定理 14-1⁺ 的形式适用与物理收支')
    # 读法：入口 i = 上游来水源（界外来水脉冲），门控单元 j = 汇点水池（水位 v，渗漏系数 λ，水位达临界 θ 时冲刷并排空），
    # 结构位 w_ij = 源 i 到汇点 j 的渠道导通（超基线部分为迹应），x_i = 源 i 近期湿润度，y_j = 汇点近期冲刷后的松散物，
    # PLUS = 冲刷扩槽（按湿润度），MINUS = 来水把松散物带入渠道淤堵（按松散物），I_j = 界外直接注水。
    M = C.frozen_snn_member()
    ok, items, der = C.check_C(M, 0, A01, 6, 2, 2)
    print(f'  取冻结常数作申报参数（无量纲化）：C_LS = {ok}，故定理 14-1⁺ 在此读法下逐字适用（形式层）')
    rng = random.Random(20261210)
    neg = tot = 0
    worst = F(0)
    letters = [(nm, (F(i0), F(0))) for nm in ['none', 'A', 'B', 'AB'] for i0 in (0, 1)]
    for _ in range(500):
        hist = FORM + [rng.choice(letters) for _ in range(40)]
        pre, recs = M.run(hist)
        L, mn = X.loose_store(M, 0, recs, 0)
        tot += 1; neg += mn < 0; worst = min(worst, mn)
    bound = M.Am * 2 * 4 * 58
    print(f'  松散物库存 L₀ = 0：500 条随机史（长 58）中库存出现负值 {neg} 条，最低 {fr(worst)}（随机史未暴露问题）')
    h = FORM + ([('none', (F(1), F(0)))] * 8 + [('B', (F(0), F(0)))] * 3 + [Q2] * 6) * 60
    pre, recs = M.run(h)
    L, mn = X.loose_store(M, 0, recs, 0)
    t_neg = next(t for t, q in enumerate(L) if q < 0)
    print(f'  对抗史（形成后重复〔注水单独冲刷 8 拍 → B 来水 3 拍不发放 → 静置 6 拍〕×60）：形成期末库存 {fr(L[18])}，首次为负在拍 {t_neg}，最低 {fr(mn)}——'
          f'B 来水把冲刷留下的松散物带入渠道（MINUS），而这些拍无冲刷补充，读法的物质收支不自动闭合')
    print(f'  充分条件：L₀ ≥ A₋·y_sup·n_in·（史长）= {fr(bound)} 时库存恒非负（逐拍淤堵 ≤ A₋·y_sup·n_in）；这是额外申报的资源前件，不由 C_LS 推出')
    print('  结论：族一ᵍ 给出形式层的非平凡非神经见证（HDyn 只经阈门控，ρ = 0），但物理构造证书（收支、能量账、本构的经验依据）未取得。')
    print()

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print('《场具化资格必然式》扩展核验（run_ext.py）')
    print()
    d0(); d1(); d2_sat(); d3(); d4(); d5(); d6(); d7(); d8()
    print('结束。')
~~~

A.14　run\_cert.py　运行入口：证书探针的包含性与可靠性、双线性对照（E1—E5）

~~~python
"""《场具化资格必然式》附录程序：证书探针的包含性与可靠性、双线性对照（E1—E5）。只用标准库，精确有理运算；种子固定。
运行（在本目录）：python3 run_cert.py
E1　K3 取等号的端点：不带严格性标记的判别漏收净探针；带严格性标记的证书收进。
E2　包含性：m-净探针全部是证书探针——随机成员覆盖 m = 0、λ = 0、λ = 1、K3 严格与等号端点；冻结变体穷尽。
E3　可靠性：证书探针上把 T 设为 b 后门控确实关闭（随机成员、对抗前缀）；「机械改 ≤」的陷阱例。
E4　覆盖统计（第十一篇随机族、冻结成员穷尽）。
E5　无门控 F 层双线性过程 c = k·x·y（k ≠ 0）的依态函数支持。
"""
import sys, os, random, itertools
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from fractions import Fraction as F
import t14_core as C
import t14_ext as X
from t14_core import fr
import run_main as RM
Q2 = ('none', (F(0), F(0)))
FORM = [('A', (F(1), F(0))), Q2, Q2] * 6
A01 = [0, 1]

def e1():
    print('E1　K3 取等号的端点：λ = 1/2、W0 = 7/16（Σ_A b = 7/8，λ³θ + Σ_A b = 1，K3 取等号）；6 次配对 + (B, I = (10, 0)) + 2 拍静置 + A 探针')
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=F(7, 16), lam=F(1, 2))
    h = FORM + [('B', (F(10), F(0))), Q2, Q2, ('A', (F(0), F(0)))]
    t = len(h) - 1; W = (t, t)
    rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A01, h, W, 6, 2, 2)
    Us = X.membrane_upper_strict(M, 0, A01, h, 15)
    v1 = X.certified_probes_nostrict(M, 0, A01, h, W, 15)
    v2 = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A01, h, W, 15)
    V = C.verify(M, 0, A01, h, W, 6, 2, 2, pairs=rg['pairs'])
    print(f'  C = {C.check_C(M, 0, A01, 6, 2, 2)[0]}；旧制度（净探针）= {rg["ok"]}，净探针 {rg["probes"]}')
    print(f'  上界递推：拍 18（B，I₀ = 10）后 (U, 严格) = ({fr(Us[19][0])}, {Us[19][1]})；拍 19 后 ({fr(Us[20][0])}, {Us[20][1]})；拍 20 后 ({fr(Us[21][0])}, {Us[21][1]})')
    print(f'  不带严格性标记的判别 {v1}（λU + Σ_A b = 1 而 U ≠ θ，漏收）；证书探针 {v2}（严格且 λ > 0，λU + Σ_A b ≤ θ 即可）；实际 T 设为 b 后探针输入 {fr(V["probe"]["vt_cf"])} < 1；R 见证 {V["R"]}')
    print()

def rand_member_eq(rng):
    """覆盖端点的随机成员：λ ∈ {0, 1, 其余}，m ∈ {0,…,4}，K3 取严格或恰等号（λ = 1 时 Σ_A b = 0）。"""
    while True:
        n_in = rng.choice([2, 3, 4]); n_out = rng.choice([1, 2])
        A = sorted(rng.sample(range(n_in), rng.randint(1, n_in - 1)))
        pats = {'none': (0,) * n_in, 'pA': tuple(1 if i in A else 0 for i in range(n_in))}
        for kk in range(rng.randint(1, 3)):
            wa = rng.choice([0, 1]); pats[f'q{kk}'] = tuple(wa if i in A else rng.choice([0, 1]) for i in range(n_in))
        th = F(rng.choice([1, 2, 3]), rng.choice([1, 2]))
        lam = rng.choice([F(0), F(1), F(rng.randint(1, 7), 8)])
        m = rng.randint(0, 4)
        room = th - lam ** (m + 1) * th
        eq = rng.random() < 0.5
        if lam == 1:
            sb = F(0)
        elif lam == 0:
            sb = th * F(rng.randint(1, 7), 8)        # λ = 0：K3 只剩 Σ_A b < θ
        else:
            sb = room if eq else room * F(rng.randint(1, 7), 8)
        if sb >= th:
            continue
        b = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        for i in A:
            b[i][0] = sb / len(A)
        for i in range(n_in):
            if i not in A:
                b[i][0] = F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8) * th
            for k in range(1, n_out):
                b[i][k] = F(rng.randint(0, 8), 8)
        hi = [[b[i][k] + F(rng.randint(1, 16), 4) * th for k in range(n_out)] for i in range(n_in)]
        lo = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
        dy = F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8); Am = F(rng.randint(0, 6), 32)
        Ap = max(Am / (1 - dy), F(1, 100)) + F(rng.randint(1, 6), 64)
        M = C.Mem(n_in, n_out, pats, [lam] * n_out, [th] * n_out, [0] * n_out, [F(rng.randint(0, 7), 8) for _ in range(n_in)],
                  [dy] * n_out, Ap, Am, 0, b, lo, hi)
        mF = rng.randint(0, 3)
        n = 1
        while not C.check_C(M, 0, A, n, mF, m)[0] and n < 300:
            n += 1
        if n >= 300:
            continue
        return M, A, n, mF, m, eq

def e2_e3():
    print('E2　包含性与 E3　可靠性：随机端点成员（λ ∈ {0, 1, 其余}，m = 0—4，K3 严格／等号）× 随机轨迹')
    rng = random.Random(20261211)
    stat = dict(mem=0, traj=0, net=0, net_in=0, cert=0, cert_ok=0, v1miss=0, lam0=0, lam1=0, eq=0, m0=0)
    for _ in range(400):
        M, A, n, mF, m, eq = rand_member_eq(rng)
        stat['mem'] += 1; stat['lam0'] += M.lam[0] == 0; stat['lam1'] += M.lam[0] == 1; stat['eq'] += eq; stat['m0'] += m == 0
        for _ in range(4):
            hist, W, tp = RM.rand_traj(rng, M, 0, A, n, mF, m, rng.randint(0, 20), rng.randint(0, 12))
            rg = C.check_regime(M, 0, A, hist, W, n, mF, m)
            pn = rg['pairs'][-1]
            c2 = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A, hist, W, pn)
            c1 = X.certified_probes_nostrict(M, 0, A, hist, W, pn)
            stat['traj'] += 1
            stat['net'] += len(rg['probes']); stat['net_in'] += sum(1 for t in rg['probes'] if t in c2)
            stat['v1miss'] += sum(1 for t in rg['probes'] if t not in c1)
            pre, recs = M.run(hist)
            for t in c2:
                o = pre[t]
                vt_cf = M.lam[0] * o[1][0] + sum(M.b[i][0] for i in A)
                stat['cert'] += 1; stat['cert_ok'] += vt_cf < M.theta[0]
    print(f'  成员 {stat["mem"]} 个（λ = 0 {stat["lam0"]}、λ = 1 {stat["lam1"]}、K3 等号 {stat["eq"]}、m = 0 {stat["m0"]}），轨迹 {stat["traj"]} 条')
    print(f'  E2：W 内净探针共 {stat["net"]} 个，其中属证书探针 {stat["net_in"]} 个；不带严格性标记的判别漏收的净探针 {stat["v1miss"]} 个')
    print(f'  E3：证书探针共 {stat["cert"]} 个，其中把 T 设为 b 后门控实参 < θ（证书可靠）{stat["cert_ok"]} 个')
    # 对抗前缀：在各种「上界可取到」的情形下核可靠性
    rng = random.Random(20261212)
    adv = adv_ok = 0
    for _ in range(400):
        M, A, n, mF, m, eq = rand_member_eq(rng)
        th = M.theta[0]; no = M.n_out
        Qr = ('none', (F(0),) * no)
        hist = []
        for k in range(n):
            hist += [Qr] * (mF if k else 0) + [('pA', (th,) + (F(0),) * (no - 1))]
        pn = len(hist) - 1
        for _ in range(rng.randint(0, 4)):
            c = rng.choice([F(0), th / 2, th - F(1, 64), th, 2 * th])
            hist.append(('none', (c,) + (F(0),) * (no - 1)))
            hist += [Qr] * rng.randint(0, 2)
        hist.append(('pA', (F(0),) * no))
        tp = len(hist) - 1
        c2 = X.certified_probes(M, 0, A, hist, (tp, tp), pn)
        if c2:
            pre, recs = M.run(hist)
            o = pre[tp]
            adv += 1; adv_ok += M.lam[0] * o[1][0] + sum(M.b[i][0] for i in A) < th
    print(f'  E3（对抗：形成后只用注入预载膜电位，再接探针）：证书探针 {adv} 个，全部可靠 {adv_ok} 个')
    # 「机械改 ≤」陷阱
    M = C.frozen_snn_member(W0=F(3, 8), lam=F(1, 2))
    c = F(1, 2)
~~~
